import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from scipy.linalg import eig, norm, inv


# ============================================================
# Part 1
# Recovery Spiral Dual Finite Model
#
# Hashimoto -> Recovery operator -> Mobius Spiral J
# ============================================================


np.set_printoptions(precision=6, suppress=True)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Regular graph construction
# ------------------------------------------------------------

N = 12          # number of vertices
degree = 3      # q+1 regular

print("\n===== Graph construction =====")

G = nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)

edges = list(G.edges())

print("vertices =", N)
print("edges    =", len(edges))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Directed edge construction
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Directed edge space =====")

directed_edges = []

for a,b in edges:
    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M = len(directed_edges)

print("directed edges =", M)


edge_index = {
    e:i for i,e in enumerate(directed_edges)
}


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Hashimoto non-backtracking matrix
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Hashimoto matrix =====")


B = np.zeros((M,M))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        # continuation
        if b == c:

            # forbid immediate return
            if d != a:
                B[i,j] = 1


print("B shape =", B.shape)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Hashimoto spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Hashimoto spectrum =====")


eig_B = eig(B)[0]

print(eig_B[:10])


spectral_radius = np.max(np.abs(eig_B))

print("\nSpectral radius")
print(spectral_radius)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Recovery operator
#
# R(u)=uB(I-uB)^(-1)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Recovery operator =====")


u = 0.15


I = np.eye(M)


R = u * B @ inv(I - u*B)


print("||R|| =", norm(R))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Spectral logarithmic coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Spiral coordinates =====")


lam = eig_B


z = np.log(np.abs(lam)+1e-12) + 1j*np.angle(lam)


for i in range(min(10,len(z))):
    print(i,z[i])


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Mobius spiral map
#
# J(z)=z/(1+i xi Re(z))
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


xi = 0.7


def mobius_spiral(z,xi):

    t = np.real(z)

    return z/(1+1j*xi*t)



z_new = mobius_spiral(z,xi)


print("\nAfter Mobius spiral")


for i in range(min(10,len(z_new))):
    print(i,z_new[i])



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Construct spectral action matrix J
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Construct J =====")


eigvals, V = eig(B)


Vinv = inv(V)


Mspiral = np.zeros((M,M),dtype=complex)


for i in range(M):

    # diagonal action
    Mspiral[i,i] = z_new[i]/z[i] if abs(z[i])>1e-12 else 1



J = V @ Mspiral @ Vinv



print("J shape =",J.shape)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Check J^2
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Duality check =====")


J2_error = norm(J@J - np.eye(M))


print("||J^2-I|| =")
print(J2_error)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Check inverse property
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n===== Inverse check =====")


inv_error = norm(J@inv(J)-np.eye(M))


print("||JJ^-1-I|| =")
print(inv_error)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. Check spectral symmetry
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Spectral symmetry =====")


RJ = J @ R @ inv(J)


dual_error = norm(
    RJ - R.conj().T
)


print("||JRJ^-1-R*||")
print(dual_error)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 12. Output summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n==============================")
print(" Recovery Spiral Test Done ")
print("==============================")

print("""
Next step:

Part 2
------
1. polar decomposition of R
2. construct R_{1/2}
3. compute H=-i log(R)
4. self-adjointness test
5. stopping kernel projection
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    eig,
    inv,
    norm,
    polar,
    logm,
    svd
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=6,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
# Part 2
#
# Recovery Spiral Dual
#
# Polar decomposition
# H_Reс = -i log(U)
# Stopping kernel
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N = 12
degree = 3


G = nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)


edges=list(G.edges())


directed_edges=[]


for a,b in edges:
    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M=len(directed_edges)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Hashimoto matrix
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros((M,M))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:
            B[i,j]=1



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")

print(B.shape)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Recovery operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.15


I=np.eye(M)


R=u*B@inv(I-u*B)



print("\n===== Recovery =====")

print("||R|| =",norm(R))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Polar decomposition
#
# R=UP
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Polar decomposition =====")


U,P=polar(R)



print("Unitary error")

print(
    norm(U.conj().T@U-I)
)


print("Positive part")

print(
    norm(P-P.conj().T)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Critical operator R_{1/2}
#
# take unitary part
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


R_half=U



print("\n===== Critical operator =====")


print(
    "||R*R-I|| = ",
    norm(
        R_half.conj().T@R_half-I
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. H_Rec=-i log(R_half)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== H_Rec =====")


H=-1j*logm(R_half)



# remove numerical noise

H=np.real_if_close(H)



print(
    "Hermitian defect"
)


herm_error=norm(
    H-H.conj().T
)


print(
    herm_error
)



# spectrum

eig_H=eig(H)[0]


print("\nH spectrum")

print(eig_H[:10])



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Spiral generator from J
#
# Construct J from polar normalized coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Spiral generator =====")


eig_B,V=eig(B)


z=np.log(
    np.abs(eig_B)+1e-12
)+1j*np.angle(eig_B)


xi=0.7


z2=z/(1+1j*xi*np.real(z))



D=np.zeros((M,M),dtype=complex)


for i in range(M):

    if abs(z[i])>1e-12:
        D[i,i]=z2[i]/z[i]
    else:
        D[i,i]=1



J=V@D@inv(V)



print(
    "J inverse defect"
)


print(
    norm(
        J@inv(J)-I
    )
)



# Spiral logarithm

Aspiral=logm(J)


print("\nSpiral generator")


print(
    "anti-Hermitian defect"
)


print(
    norm(
        Aspiral+Aspiral.conj().T
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Stopping kernel
#
# ker(I-R)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Stopping kernel =====")


S=I-R


U_s,sigma,Vh=svd(S)


tol=1e-10


kernel_dim=np.sum(
    sigma<tol
)


print(
    "kernel dimension =",
    kernel_dim
)


print(
    "small singular values"
)


print(
    sigma[-10:]
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Check J preservation
#
# J K = K
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


if kernel_dim>0:

    K=Vh.conj().T[:,-kernel_dim:]

    error_JK=norm(
        J@K-K
    )

    print(
        "J kernel preservation =",
        error_JK
    )


else:

    print(
        "No stopping kernel detected"
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("""
============================
 Part 2 Complete
============================

Computed:

1. Polar decomposition R=UP

2. Critical operator R_half

3. H_Rec=-i log(R_half)

4. Hermitian defect

5. Spiral generator log(J)

6. Stopping kernel dimension


Next Part 3:

- Tune xi parameter
- Optimize Mobius dual
- Force J^2=I
- Compare J-fixed space with ker(I-R)
- Search spectral zero condition

""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    eig,
    inv,
    norm,
    polar,
    svd,
    logm
)

from scipy.optimize import minimize_scalar


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=8,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
# Part 3
#
# Mobius Spiral Dual Optimization
#
# J_xi^2 ~ I
# JRJ^-1 ~ R*
# Fixed space comparison
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N = 12
degree = 3


G = nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)


edges=list(G.edges())


directed_edges=[]

for a,b in edges:
    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M=len(directed_edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Hashimoto matrix
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros((M,M))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:
            B[i,j]=1



I=np.eye(M)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Recovery operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.15


R=u*B@inv(I-u*B)



# Critical operator

U,P=polar(R)

R_half=U



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Spectral coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


eig_B,V=eig(B)

Vinv=inv(V)


z=np.log(
    np.abs(eig_B)+1e-12
)+1j*np.angle(eig_B)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Mobius spiral J
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def build_J(xi):

    z2=z/(1+1j*xi*np.real(z))

    D=np.eye(M,dtype=complex)

    for i in range(M):

        if abs(z[i])>1e-12:
            D[i,i]=z2[i]/z[i]


    J=V@D@Vinv

    return J



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Error function
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def errors(xi):

    J=build_J(xi)

    e1=norm(
        J@J-I
    )


    e2=norm(
        J@R@inv(J)-R.conj().T
    )


    return e1+e2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Optimize xi
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Optimize xi =====")


result=minimize_scalar(
    errors,
    bounds=(-5,5),
    method="bounded"
)


xi_opt=result.x


print("xi optimum")
print(xi_opt)


print("error")
print(result.fun)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Construct optimal J
# ------------------------------------------------------------


J=build_J(xi_opt)



print("\n===== Optimized J =====")


print(
    "||J^2-I||"
)

print(
    norm(J@J-I)
)


print(
    "||JRJ^-1-R*||"
)

print(
    norm(
        J@R@inv(J)-R.conj().T
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Fixed space of J
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== J fixed space =====")


Uj,sj,Vhj=svd(J-I)


tol=1e-10


dim_J=np.sum(sj<tol)


print(
    "dim ker(J-I)=",
    dim_J
)


print(
    sj[-10:]
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Critical fixed space
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Critical kernel =====")


Ur,sr,Vhr=svd(R_half-I)



dim_R=np.sum(sr<tol)


print(
    "dim ker(R_half-I)=",
    dim_R
)


print(
    sr[-10:]
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. H_Rec
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== H_Rec =====")


H=-1j*logm(R_half)


print(
    "Hermitian defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        H-H.conj().T
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 12. Compare spaces
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("""
=============================
 Part 3 Completed
=============================


Computed:

1. Optimal Mobius parameter xi

2. J^2-I

3. JRJ^-1-R*

4. J fixed space

5. Critical fixed space

6. H_Rec self-adjointness


Next:

Part 4

- Replace J by exact involution
- Construct J-preserving stopping kernel
- Compare with zero spectrum

""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    eig,
    inv,
    norm,
    polar,
    svd,
    logm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=8,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
# Part 4
#
# Exact spectral involution J
#
# JUJ^-1 = U*
# J^2 = I
#
# Compare stopping kernel
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N=12
degree=3


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)


edges=list(G.edges())


directed_edges=[]


for a,b in edges:

    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M=len(directed_edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Hashimoto
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros((M,M))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



I=np.eye(M)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.15


R=u*B@inv(I-u*B)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Critical operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


U,P=polar(R)


R_half=U



print("\n===== Critical operator =====")


print(
    "unitary defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        U.conj().T@U-I
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Spectral decomposition of U
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Spectrum U =====")


eigU,V=eig(U)


for x in eigU[:10]:

    print(x)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Construct exact involution J
#
# pair eigenvalues e^{iθ}, e^{-iθ}
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Construct involution =====")


J=np.zeros(
    (M,M),
    dtype=complex
)


used=np.zeros(M,bool)


for i in range(M):

    if used[i]:
        continue


    lam=eigU[i]


    # find conjugate inverse eigenvalue

    target=np.conj(lam)


    dist=[
        abs(eigU[j]-target)
        for j in range(M)
    ]


    j=np.argmin(dist)


    used[i]=True
    used[j]=True


    vi=V[:,i]
    vj=V[:,j]


    # swap operator

    J += np.outer(vj,vi.conj())

    J += np.outer(vi,vj.conj())



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Check involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== J checks =====")


print(
    "||J^2-I||"
)

print(
    norm(
        J@J-I
    )
)



print(
    "||JUJ^-1-U*||"
)

print(
    norm(
        J@U@inv(J)-U.conj().T
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. J fixed space
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== J fixed space =====")


Uj,sj,Vhj=svd(J-I)


dimJ=np.sum(
    sj<1e-10
)


print(
    "dim ker(J-I)=",
    dimJ
)


print(
    sj[-10:]
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Critical fixed space
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Critical fixed space =====")


Uc,sc,Vhc=svd(U-I)


dimU=np.sum(
    sc<1e-10
)


print(
    "dim ker(U-I)=",
    dimU
)


print(
    sc[-10:]
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Compare spaces
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Compare =====")


if dimJ>0 and dimU>0:

    print(
        """
Non-trivial fixed spaces detected.
Next:
compare basis overlap.
"""
    )


else:

    print(
        """
No common fixed space.
"""
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. H_Rec
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== H_Rec =====")


H=-1j*logm(U)


print(
    "Hermitian defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        H-H.conj().T
    )
)



print("""
================================
 Part 4 Complete
================================


Constructed:

1. Exact involution J

2. J^2=I test

3. JUJ^-1=U* test

4. J fixed space

5. Critical fixed space

6. H_Rec


Next Part 5:

- Recover spectral zeros from H
- Compare fixed spectrum
- Introduce Satake parameters

""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    eig,
    inv,
    norm,
    polar,
    logm,
    svd
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=8,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
# Part 5
#
# Recovery spectral zero experiment
#
# H_Rec spectrum
# zero modes
# Satake correspondence
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N=12
degree=3


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)


edges=list(G.edges())


directed_edges=[]


for a,b in edges:

    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M=len(directed_edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Hashimoto matrix
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros((M,M))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



I=np.eye(M)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Recovery operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.15


R=u*B@inv(I-u*B)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Critical unitary operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


U,P=polar(R)



print("\n===== Unit check =====")

print(
    norm(U.conj().T@U-I)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. H_Rec
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== H_Rec =====")


H=-1j*logm(U)


H=np.real_if_close(H)



print(
    "Hermitian defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        H-H.conj().T
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. H spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== H spectrum =====")


gamma=eig(H)[0]


gamma=np.real(gamma)


gamma=np.sort(gamma)



for x in gamma:

    print(
        x
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Zero modes
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Zero spectrum =====")


zero_tol=1e-10


zero_modes=np.sum(
    np.abs(gamma)<zero_tol
)


print(
    "number of zero modes="
)

print(
    zero_modes
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Symmetry gamma -> -gamma
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Spectral symmetry =====")


sym_error=[]


for g in gamma:

    sym_error.append(
        np.min(
            np.abs(gamma+g)
        )
    )


print(
    "mean symmetry error"
)

print(
    np.mean(sym_error)
)



print(
    "max symmetry error"
)

print(
    np.max(sym_error)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Hashimoto -> Satake parameters
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake map =====")


lam=eig(B)[0]


q=degree-1


satake=[]


for x in lam:

    disc=x*x-4*q

    a=(x+np.sqrt(disc))/2

    b=(x-np.sqrt(disc))/2

    satake.append(
        (a,b)
    )



for i in range(10):

    print(
        lam[i],
        "->",
        satake[i]
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Compare H spectrum and Satake phase
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Phase comparison =====")


phase=[]


for a,b in satake:

    phase.append(
        np.angle(a)
    )



phase=np.sort(
    np.array(phase)
)


print(
    phase[:10]
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. Stopping kernel at criticality
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Critical stopping kernel =====")


Uc,sc,Vhc=svd(U-I)


dim=np.sum(
    sc<1e-10
)


print(
    "dim ker(U-I)=",
    dim
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("""
================================

 Part 5 Complete

Computed:

1. H_Rec spectrum

2. zero modes

3. ± spectral symmetry

4. Satake parameters

5. critical stopping kernel


Next Part 6:

- Correct involution J construction
- Verify JUJ^{-1}=U*
- Compare J-fixed and zero modes

================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    eig,
    inv,
    norm,
    polar,
    logm,
    schur,
    svd
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=8,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
# Part 6
#
# Exact spectral dual involution
#
# JUJ^{-1}=U*
# Compare:
# ker(J-I)
# ker(U-I)
# ker(H)
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Hashimoto construction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N=12
degree=3


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)


edges=list(G.edges())


directed_edges=[]


for a,b in edges:
    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M=len(directed_edges)



B=np.zeros((M,M))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:
            B[i,j]=1



I=np.eye(M)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Recovery operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.15


R=u*B@inv(I-u*B)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Critical unitary part
# ------------------------------------------------------------


U,P=polar(R)



print("\n===== U unitary =====")

print(
    norm(U.conj().T@U-I)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Schur decomposition
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Schur decomposition =====")


T,Q=schur(
    U,
    output='complex'
)


D=np.diag(T)



print(D[:10])



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Build exact dual in Schur basis
#
# theta -> -theta
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Construct J =====")


theta=np.angle(D)



JD=np.zeros(
    (M,M),
    dtype=complex
)



used=np.zeros(M,bool)


for i in range(M):

    if used[i]:
        continue


    target=-theta[i]


    j=np.argmin(
        np.abs(
            np.angle(
                np.exp(1j*theta)
            )-target
        )
    )


    used[i]=True
    used[j]=True


    JD[j,i]=1
    JD[i,j]=1



# Back transform

J=Q@JD@Q.conj().T



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Checks
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== J checks =====")


print(
    "J^2-I"
)

print(
    norm(
        J@J-I
    )
)



print(
    "JUJ^-1-U*"
)

print(
    norm(
        J@U@inv(J)-U.conj().T
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. J fixed space
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== J fixed =====")


_,sj,_=svd(J-I)


dimJ=np.sum(
    sj<1e-10
)


print(
    "dim ker(J-I)=",
    dimJ
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. U fixed space
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== U fixed =====")


_,su,_=svd(U-I)


dimU=np.sum(
    su<1e-10
)


print(
    "dim ker(U-I)=",
    dimU
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. H spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== H =====")


H=-1j*logm(U)


print(
    "Hermitian defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        H-H.conj().T
    )
)



_,sH,_=svd(H)


dimH=np.sum(
    sH<1e-10
)


print(
    "dim ker(H)=",
    dimH
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Compare J action on zero space
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Zero space invariance =====")


U0,s0,V0=svd(H)


r=np.sum(s0<1e-10)


if r>0:

    Z=V0.conj().T[:,-r:]

    error=norm(
        J@Z-Z
    )

    print(
        "||JZ-Z||="
    )

    print(error)


else:

    print(
        "No zero modes"
    )



print("""
====================================

 Part 6 Complete

Obtained:

1. Exact involution candidate J

2. J^2=I

3. JUJ^-1=U*

4. J fixed dimension

5. U fixed dimension

6. H zero modes

7. J invariance of zero space


Next Part 7:

Recovery trace formula
and spectral determinant

====================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    inv,
    polar,
    eig,
    logm,
    det
)

from numpy.linalg import slogdet


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
#
# Part 7
#
# Recovery trace formula
# Spectral determinant
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N=12
degree=3


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)


edges=list(G.edges())


directed_edges=[]


for a,b in edges:

    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M=len(directed_edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Hashimoto matrix
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (M,M)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



I=np.eye(M)



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")

print(B.shape)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Recovery operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.15


R=u*B@inv(I-u*B)



print("\n===== Recovery =====")

print(
    "norm R"
)

print(
    np.linalg.norm(R)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Trace expansion
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Trace formula =====")


for n in range(1,11):

    tr=np.trace(
        np.linalg.matrix_power(R,n)
    )

    print(
        n,
        tr
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Recovery zeta
#
# Z(s)=det(I-sR)^-1
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Recovery determinant =====")


for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.5,
    0.8
]:

    sign,ld=slogdet(
        I-s*R
    )

    Z=np.exp(
        -ld
    )

    print(
        "s=",
        s,
        " Z=",
        Z
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Polar critical operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


U,P=polar(R)



H=-1j*logm(U)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Spectral determinant
#
# Xi(z)=det(zI-H)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Xi Recovery =====")


gamma=eig(H)[0]


gamma=np.real_if_close(gamma)



for z in [
    0,
    0.5,
    1,
    2
]:

    Xi=det(
        z*np.eye(M)-H
    )

    print(
        "z=",
        z,
        Xi
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Zero spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Zero modes =====")


vals=np.real(
    eig(H)[0]
)


zeros=np.sum(
    np.abs(vals)<1e-10
)


print(
    "zero multiplicity"
)

print(
    zeros
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Compare trace and eigen expansion
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Spectral trace =====")


lam=eig(R)[0]


for n in range(1,6):

    a=np.trace(
        np.linalg.matrix_power(R,n)
    )

    b=np.sum(
        lam**n
    )

    print(
        n,
        abs(a-b)
    )



print("""
======================================

 Part 7 Complete

Computed:

1. Recovery traces

2. Fredholm determinant

3. Recovery zeta

4. H spectrum determinant

5. Zero multiplicity

6. Trace/eigenvalue identity


Next Part 8:

- Bass compression
- vertex reduction
- Satake polynomial comparison

======================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    inv,
    det,
    eig,
    norm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
#
# Part 8
#
# Recovery Bass Compression
#
# Edge space -> Vertex space
#
# Hashimoto B
# adjacency A
# Satake polynomial
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)



print("\n===== Graph =====")

print(
    "vertices",
    N
)

print(
    "edges",
    G.number_of_edges()
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Directed edges
# ------------------------------------------------------------


edges=list(G.edges())


directed_edges=[]


for a,b in edges:

    directed_edges.append((a,b))
    directed_edges.append((b,a))


M=len(directed_edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Hashimoto B
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (M,M)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed_edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed_edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")

print(
    B.shape
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Vertex adjacency A
# ------------------------------------------------------------


A=nx.to_numpy_array(
    G
)


print("\n===== Adjacency =====")

print(
    A.shape
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Bass polynomial
#
# det(I-uB)
# =
# (1-u^2)^(m-n)
# det(I-uA+qu^2I)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Bass identity =====")


u=0.2


left=det(
    np.eye(M)-u*B
)


right=(
    (1-u*u)**(
        G.number_of_edges()-N
    )
    *
    det(
        np.eye(N)
        -u*A
        +q*u*u*np.eye(N)
    )
)


print(
    "det(I-uB)"
)

print(left)


print(
    "Bass RHS"
)

print(right)


print(
    "error"
)

print(
    abs(left-right)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Recovery edge operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


R_edge=(
    u*B
    @
    inv(
        np.eye(M)-u*B
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Recovery vertex compression
# ------------------------------------------------------------


R_vertex=(
    u*A
    @
    inv(
        np.eye(N)
        -u*A
        +q*u*u*np.eye(N)
    )
)



print("\n===== Recovery compression =====")


print(
    "edge dimension"
)

print(
    R_edge.shape
)


print(
    "vertex dimension"
)

print(
    R_vertex.shape
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Spectral comparison
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Spectrum edge =====")


lam_edge=eig(R_edge)[0]


for x in lam_edge[:10]:

    print(x)



print("\n===== Spectrum vertex =====")


lam_vertex=eig(R_vertex)[0]


for x in lam_vertex:

    print(x)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Satake polynomial
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake =====")


lamA=eig(A)[0]


for x in lamA[:10]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -x,
            q
        ]
    )

    print(
        "A eigenvalue",
        x,
        "=>",
        roots
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("""
======================================

 Part 8 Complete

Computed:

1. Hashimoto operator

2. Adjacency operator

3. Bass determinant identity

4. Edge Recovery operator

5. Vertex compressed operator

6. Satake quadratic relation


Next Part 9:

Recovery Bass theorem

- prove compression map
- construct vertex Green function
- connect Satake parameters

======================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    inv,
    det,
    eig,
    norm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
#
# Part 9
#
# Recovery Bass Theorem Numerical Verification
#
# Green function compression
# Trace identity
# Euler determinant comparison
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


N=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    N,
    seed=42
)


m=G.number_of_edges()


print("\n===== Graph =====")

print(
    "n=",
    N
)

print(
    "m=",
    m
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Directed edge space
# ------------------------------------------------------------


directed=[]


for a,b in G.edges():

    directed.append((a,b))
    directed.append((b,a))


M=len(directed)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Hashimoto
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (M,M)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Adjacency
# ------------------------------------------------------------


A=nx.to_numpy_array(
    G
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Green functions
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.2


GB=inv(
    np.eye(M)-u*B
)


GA=inv(
    np.eye(N)
    -
    u*A
    +
    q*u*u*np.eye(N)
)



print("\n===== Green =====")


print(
    "GB",
    GB.shape
)


print(
    "GA",
    GA.shape
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Trace Bass relation
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Trace identity =====")


lhs=np.trace(GB)


rhs=(
    np.trace(GA)
    +
    (m-N)
    *
    (
        2*u/(1-u*u)
    )
)


print(
    "Tr GB"
)

print(lhs)


print(
    "compressed"
)

print(rhs)


print(
    "error"
)

print(
    abs(lhs-rhs)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Recovery Bass kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Recovery Bass kernel =====")


K=u*A@GA


print(
    "K dimension"
)

print(
    K.shape
)


print(
    "norm K"
)

print(
    norm(K)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Determinant
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Bass zeta =====")


Z_Bass=1/det(
    np.eye(N)-K
)


print(
    "Z_Bass"
)

print(Z_Bass)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Compare with Hashimoto zeta
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Hashimoto zeta =====")


Z_H=1/det(
    np.eye(M)-u*B
)


print(
    "Z_H"
)

print(Z_H)



# Bass correction

correction=(
    (1-u*u)**(
        N-m
    )
)



print(
    "Corrected"
)

print(
    correction*Z_H
)



print(
    "difference"
)

print(
    abs(
        correction*Z_H-Z_Bass
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Satake spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake spectrum =====")


mu=eig(A)[0]


for x in mu[:10]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -x,
            q
        ]
    )

    print(
        x,
        "=>",
        roots
    )



print("""
====================================

 Part 9 Complete


Verified:

1. Hashimoto Green function

2. Bass compressed Green function

3. Trace correction

4. Recovery Bass kernel

5. Euler determinant relation

6. Satake quadratic


Next Part 10:

Recovery Bass Compression Theorem

- operator proof
- kernel decomposition
- q>1 recursive kernel

====================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    inv,
    det,
    eig,
    norm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
#
# Part 10
#
# Recovery Bass Compression Theorem
#
# Correct resolvent formulation
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------


n=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    n,
    seed=42
)


m=G.number_of_edges()


print("\n===== Graph =====")

print("n =",n)
print("m =",m)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Directed edges
# ------------------------------------------------------------


edges=[]

for a,b in G.edges():

    edges.append((a,b))
    edges.append((b,a))


E=len(edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Hashimoto
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (E,E)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Incidence maps
#
# D : vertex -> edge
# C : edge -> vertex
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


D=np.zeros(
    (E,n)
)


C=np.zeros(
    (n,E)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    D[i,a]=1

    C[b,i]=1



print("\n===== Compression maps =====")

print(
    "C",
    C.shape
)

print(
    "D",
    D.shape
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Check C D
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== CD check =====")


print(
    C@D
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Resolvent compression
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.2


RE=inv(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)


compressed=C@RE@D


print("\n===== Compressed resolvent =====")


print(
    compressed.shape
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Bass vertex operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


A=nx.to_numpy_array(G)


M=(
    np.eye(n)
    -
    u*A
    +
    q*u*u*np.eye(n)
)


RV=inv(M)



print("\n===== Vertex Green =====")


print(
    RV.shape
)


print(
    "compression error"
)


print(
    norm(
        compressed-RV
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Log derivative trace
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Log derivative =====")


edge_trace=np.trace(
    B@inv(
        np.eye(E)-u*B
    )
)


vertex_trace=np.trace(
    (A-2*q*u*np.eye(n))
    @
    inv(M)
)


correction=(
    2*(m-n)*u/(1-u*u)
)


print(
    "edge"
)

print(edge_trace)


print(
    "vertex + correction"
)

print(
    vertex_trace
    +
    correction
)


print(
    "error"
)

print(
    abs(
        edge_trace
        -
        vertex_trace
        -
        correction
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. q>1 Recursive kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Recursive kernel =====")


lam=eig(A)[0]


for L in lam[:8]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            q,
            -L,
            1
        ]
    )

    w=roots[0]


    print(
        "lambda=",
        L,
        "w=",
        w
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Satake parameter
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake =====")


for L in lam[:8]:

    alpha,beta=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -L,
            q
        ]
    )

    print(
        L,
        "=>",
        alpha,
        beta
    )



print("""
======================================

 Part 10 Complete


Computed:

1. Edge-to-vertex compression map

2. Hashimoto resolvent

3. Bass vertex Green function

4. Log determinant derivative

5. q>1 recursive kernel

6. Satake parameters


Next Part 11:

Recovery Bass Compression Theorem

- abstract operator proof
- Fredholm determinant
- Recovery zeta construction

======================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    inv,
    det,
    eig,
    norm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
#
# Part 11
#
# Recovery Bass Compression
#
# Fredholm determinant
# Kernel decomposition
# Recursive q>1 kernel
#
# ============================================================


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Graph
# ------------------------------------------------------------

n=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    n,
    seed=42
)

m=G.number_of_edges()



print("\n===== Graph =====")

print("n=",n)
print("m=",m)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Directed edges
# ------------------------------------------------------------


edges=[]

for a,b in G.edges():

    edges.append((a,b))
    edges.append((b,a))


E=len(edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Hashimoto
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros((E,E))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Incidence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


C=np.zeros((n,E))
D=np.zeros((E,n))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    C[b,i]=1
    D[i,a]=1



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Adjacency
# ------------------------------------------------------------


A=nx.to_numpy_array(G)



# ============================================================
# 1. Recovery operators
# ============================================================


u=0.2


RB = (
    u*B
    @
    inv(
        np.eye(E)-u*B
    )
)


RA = (
    u*A
    @
    inv(
        np.eye(n)
        -
        u*A
        +
        q*u*u*np.eye(n)
    )
)



print("\n===== Recovery operators =====")


print(
    "RB",
    RB.shape
)

print(
    "RA",
    RA.shape
)



# ============================================================
# 2. Fredholm determinant
# ============================================================


print("\n===== Fredholm determinant =====")


ZB=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-RB
)


ZA=1/det(
    np.eye(n)-RA
)


print(
    "Z edge"
)

print(ZB)


print(
    "Z vertex"
)

print(ZA)



# ============================================================
# 3. Bass correction determinant
# ============================================================


corr=(1-u*u)**(
    m-n
)


print(
    "corrected vertex"
)

print(
    corr*ZB
)



print(
    "det error"
)

print(
    abs(
        corr*ZB-ZA
    )
)



# ============================================================
# 4. Kernel decomposition
# ============================================================


print("\n===== Kernel decomposition =====")


sC=np.linalg.svd(C)[1]


rankC=np.sum(
    sC>1e-10
)


dim_kernel=E-rankC


print(
    "rank C",
    rankC
)


print(
    "dim ker C",
    dim_kernel
)



# Tree sector

print(
    "tree dimension",
    rankC
)


print(
    "loop dimension",
    dim_kernel
)



# ============================================================
# 5. Projectors
# ============================================================


U,S,Vh=np.linalg.svd(C)


P_tree=(
    Vh[:rankC,:].T
    @
    Vh[:rankC,:]
)


P_loop=np.eye(E)-P_tree



print("\n===== Projectors =====")


print(
    "tree defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        P_tree@P_tree-P_tree
    )
)


print(
    "loop defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        P_loop@P_loop-P_loop
    )
)



# ============================================================
# 6. Loop / Tree trace split
# ============================================================


print("\n===== Trace split =====")


trace_tree=np.trace(
    P_tree@RB
)


trace_loop=np.trace(
    P_loop@RB
)


print(
    "tree trace",
    trace_tree
)


print(
    "loop trace",
    trace_loop
)


print(
    "total",
    trace_tree+trace_loop
)



# ============================================================
# 7. q>1 recursive kernel
# ============================================================


print("\n===== q recursion =====")


lam=eig(A)[0]


for L in lam[:5]:

    w=0.1+0j

    print(
        "\nlambda",
        L
    )

    for k in range(10):

        w=1/(L-q*w)

        print(
            k,
            w
        )



# ============================================================
# 8. Satake
# ============================================================


print("\n===== Satake =====")


for L in lam[:5]:

    alpha,beta=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -L,
            q
        ]
    )

    print(
        L,
        alpha,
        beta
    )



print("""
======================================

 Part 11 Complete


Computed:

1. Recovery Fredholm operator

2. Bass determinant comparison

3. Loop/Tree kernel decomposition

4. Projection operators

5. Trace splitting

6. q>1 recursive kernel

7. Satake parameters


Next Part 12:

Recovery Bass theorem formalization

- abstract Hilbert decomposition
- invariant kernel
- Euler product construction

======================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    eig,
    det,
    norm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
#
# Part 12 corrected
#
# Recovery Bass theorem
#
# Hilbert decomposition
# invariant kernel
# Euler factorization
#
# ============================================================


n=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    n,
    seed=42
)


m=G.number_of_edges()


print("\n===== Graph =====")
print("n =",n)
print("m =",m)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Directed edges
# ------------------------------------------------------------


edges=[]

for a,b in G.edges():

    edges.append((a,b))
    edges.append((b,a))


E=len(edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Hashimoto
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (E,E)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")
print(B.shape)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Incidence maps
# ------------------------------------------------------------


C=np.zeros((n,E))
D=np.zeros((E,n))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    C[b,i]=1

    D[i,a]=1



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Moore Penrose projection
# ------------------------------------------------------------


CD=C@D


CD_pinv=np.linalg.pinv(CD)


Pi_tree=(
    D
    @
    CD_pinv
    @
    C
)


Pi_loop=np.eye(E)-Pi_tree



print("\n===== Projection =====")


print(
    "tree defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        Pi_tree@Pi_tree-Pi_tree
    )
)


print(
    "loop defect"
)

print(
    norm(
        Pi_loop@Pi_loop-Pi_loop
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# ranks
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Dimension =====")


print(
    "tree",
    np.linalg.matrix_rank(Pi_tree)
)


print(
    "loop",
    np.linalg.matrix_rank(Pi_loop)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Operators
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B_tree=(
    Pi_tree
    @
    B
    @
    Pi_tree
)


B_loop=(
    Pi_loop
    @
    B
    @
    Pi_loop
)



print("\n===== Spectra =====")


print("tree")

for x in eig(B_tree)[0][:10]:

    print(x)


print("loop")

for x in eig(B_loop)[0][:10]:

    print(x)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Determinant
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Euler factor =====")


u=0.2


Z_full=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)


Z_tree=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-u*B_tree
)


Z_loop=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-u*B_loop
)



print(
    "Z full",
    Z_full
)


print(
    "Z tree*loop",
    Z_tree*Z_loop
)


print(
    "factor error"
)

print(
    abs(
        Z_full-Z_tree*Z_loop
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Stopping kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== invariant kernel =====")


s=np.linalg.svd(
    np.eye(E)-B_loop
)[1]


print(
    "singular values"
)

print(
    s[:10]
)


print(
    "kernel dimension"
)

print(
    np.sum(
        s<1e-10
    )
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recursive kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== q recursion =====")


A=nx.to_numpy_array(G)


for lam in eig(A)[0][:5]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            q,
            -lam,
            1
        ]
    )

    print(
        lam,
        roots
    )



print("""
======================================

Part 12 corrected complete


Computed:

1. Moore-Penrose Bass projection

2. Hilbert decomposition

3. Loop invariant sector

4. Tree invariant sector

5. Euler determinant factorization

6. Stopping kernel

7. q>1 recursive roots


Next Part 13:

Recovery Bass theorem proof structure

- exact edge/vertex intertwining
- Euler product
- L-function extension

======================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import eig, det, norm


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# ============================================================
#
# Part 13
#
# Recovery Bass theorem
#
# Intertwining construction
#
# ============================================================


n=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    n,
    seed=42
)


m=G.number_of_edges()


print("\n===== Graph =====")
print("n =",n)
print("m =",m)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# directed edge
# ------------------------------------------------------------


edges=[]

for a,b in G.edges():

    edges.append((a,b))
    edges.append((b,a))


E=len(edges)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Hashimoto
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (E,E)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")
print(B.shape)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Incidence maps
#
# C : edge -> vertex (terminal)
# D : vertex -> edge (origin)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


C=np.zeros(
    (n,E)
)


D=np.zeros(
    (E,n)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    C[b,i]=1
    D[i,a]=1



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Bass compressed operator
#
# A = C B D
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


A_rec=C@B@D


print("\n===== Recovery vertex operator =====")
print(A_rec.shape)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Intertwining test
#
# B D ?= D A
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


left=B@D
right=D@A_rec


print("\n===== Intertwining =====")


print(
"||BD-DA||"
)

print(
norm(left-right)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Correct normalized compression
#
# q+1 regular:
#
# B^2 relation
#
# A = C B D /(q)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


A_vertex=A_rec/q


print("\n===== Vertex operator =====")

print(
eig(A_vertex)[0][:10]
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Bass quadratic
#
# lambda_B satisfies:
#
# lambda^2 - mu lambda + q =0
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake recovery =====")


for mu in eig(A_vertex)[0][:8]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -mu,
            q
        ]
    )

    print(
        "mu=",
        mu,
        "=>",
        roots
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Construct invariant edge space
#
# generated by D(V)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


P=np.real(
    D@
    np.linalg.pinv(D.T@D)
    @
    D.T
)


print("\n===== Edge projector =====")


print(
"defect"
)

print(
norm(P@P-P)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Reduced Hashimoto
#
# PBP
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


B_red=P@B@P


print("\n===== Reduced spectrum =====")


for x in eig(B_red)[0][:12]:

    print(x)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# determinant comparison
#
# Bass corrected form
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


u=0.2


Z_edge=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)


M=np.eye(n)-u*A_vertex+q*u*u*np.eye(n)


Z_bass=(
(1-u*u)**(-(E-n))
/
det(M)
)



print("\n===== Bass determinant =====")


print(
"edge"
)

print(Z_edge)


print(
"bass"
)

print(Z_bass)


print(
"error"
)

print(
abs(Z_edge-Z_bass)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Euler product local factors
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Euler local factor =====")


mu=eig(A_vertex)[0]


for x in mu[:5]:

    alpha=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -x,
            q
        ]
    )

    print(
        alpha
    )



print("""
======================================

Part 13 Complete


Computed:

1. Recovery vertex operator C B D

2. Edge/vertex intertwining

3. Invariant edge subspace

4. Satake quadratic recovery

5. Bass determinant formula

6. Euler local parameters


Next Part 14:

- Exact Recovery Bass compression theorem
- q>1 recursive Green function
- Fredholm determinant of compressed operator

======================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import det, inv, eig, norm


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# =====================================================
#
# Part 14
#
# Recovery Bass theorem
#
# Resolvent compression
#
# =====================================================


n=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    n,
    seed=42
)


print("\n===== Graph =====")

print(
"n=",
n
)

print(
"m=",
G.number_of_edges()
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Directed edges
# -----------------------------------------------------


edges=[]

for a,b in G.edges():

    edges.append((a,b))
    edges.append((b,a))


E=len(edges)



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Hashimoto
# -----------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (E,E)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")
print(B.shape)



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Incidence
# -----------------------------------------------------


C=np.zeros((n,E))

D=np.zeros((E,n))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    C[b,i]=1
    D[i,a]=1



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Resolvent
#
# G_B(u)=(I-uB)^-1
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


u=0.2


GB=inv(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)


print("\n===== Edge Green =====")

print(
GB.shape
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Vertex compressed Green
#
# CGBD
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


GV=C@GB@D


print("\n===== Compressed Green =====")

print(
GV.shape
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Bass vertex formula
#
# (I-uA+qu^2I)^-1
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


A=nx.to_numpy_array(G)


GV_bass=inv(
    np.eye(n)
    -
    u*A
    +
    q*u*u*np.eye(n)
)



print("\n===== Bass Green =====")


print(
"error"
)


print(
norm(
GV/q-GV_bass
)
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Determinant identity
#
# det(I-uB)
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


Z_edge=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)


Z_bass=(
(1-u*u)**(-(E-n))
/
det(
    np.eye(n)
    -
    u*A
    +
    q*u*u*np.eye(n)
)
)



print("\n===== Bass determinant =====")


print(
"edge",
Z_edge
)

print(
"bass",
Z_bass
)

print(
"error",
abs(Z_edge-Z_bass)
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# q recursion
#
# w=1/(lambda-qw)
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== q recursion =====")


for lam in eig(A)[0][:8]:

    w=0.0+0j


    for k in range(30):

        w=1/(lam-q*w)


    print(
        "lambda",
        lam,
        "w",
        w
    )



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Satake parameters
#
# lambda = alpha+beta
# alpha beta=q
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake =====")


for lam in eig(A)[0][:8]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -lam,
            q
        ]
    )


    print(
        lam,
        "=>",
        roots
    )



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Euler local factor
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Euler factor =====")


for alpha_beta in [
    np.roots([1,-eig(A)[0][0],q])
]:

    alpha,beta=alpha_beta

    print(
        (1-u*alpha)**(-1)
        *
        (1-u*beta)**(-1)
    )



print("""
=====================================

Part 14 Complete


Computed:

1. Hashimoto resolvent

2. Recovery Green compression

3. Bass vertex Green function

4. Fredholm determinant identity

5. q>1 recursive Green equation

6. Satake parameters

7. Euler local factor


Next Part 15:

Recovery Bass Compression Theorem

- abstract proof
- stopping kernel
- L-function Fredholm determinant

=====================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    inv,
    det,
    eig,
    norm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# =====================================================
#
# Part 15
#
# Recovery Bass Compression
#
# corrected Green kernel
#
# =====================================================


n=12
degree=3
q=degree-1


G=nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    n,
    seed=42
)


edges=[]

for a,b in G.edges():

    edges.append((a,b))
    edges.append((b,a))


E=len(edges)



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Hashimoto
# -----------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (E,E)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")

print(B.shape)



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Incidence
# -----------------------------------------------------


C=np.zeros(
    (n,E)
)

D=np.zeros(
    (E,n)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    C[b,i]=1
    D[i,a]=1



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Green
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


u=0.2


GE=inv(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)



GV=C@GE@D



print("\n===== Green compression =====")

print(GV.shape)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Backtracking correction
#
# C D = degree matrix
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


CD=C@D



print("\n===== CD =====")

print(CD)



# regular graph correction

Deg=degree*np.eye(n)


GV_corr=(
    GV
    -
    u*u*
    Deg
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Bass vertex kernel
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


A=nx.to_numpy_array(G)


GB=inv(
    np.eye(n)
    -
    u*A
    +
    q*u*u*np.eye(n)
)



print("\n===== Bass Green =====")


print(
"raw error"
)

print(
norm(GV-GB)
)


print(
"corrected error"
)

print(
norm(GV_corr-GB)
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# determinant
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


Z_edge=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)


Z_bass=(
(1-u*u)**(-(E-n))
/
det(
np.eye(n)
-u*A
+q*u*u*np.eye(n)
)
)



print("\n===== determinant =====")

print(
"edge",
Z_edge
)

print(
"bass",
Z_bass
)

print(
"error",
abs(Z_edge-Z_bass)
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# stopping kernel
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== stopping kernel =====")


s=np.linalg.svd(
    np.eye(E)-B
)[1]


print(
s[:15]
)


print(
"dim"
)

print(
np.sum(s<1e-10)
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Fredholm compressed operator
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


T=u*B


print("\n===== Fredholm =====")


print(
det(
np.eye(E)-T
)
)


# vertex Fredholm


T_v=u*A-q*u*u*np.eye(n)


print(
det(
np.eye(n)-T_v
)
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Satake
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake =====")


for lam in eig(A)[0][:8]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -lam,
            q
        ]
    )

    print(
        lam,
        roots
    )



print("""
=====================================

Part 15 Complete


Computed:

1. Corrected Green compression

2. Backtracking correction

3. Bass Fredholm determinant

4. Stopping kernel

5. Compressed Fredholm operator

Next Part 16:

Recovery Bass Compression Theorem

- rigorous operator formulation
- q>1 recursive kernel
- Recovery zeta determinant

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx

from scipy.linalg import (
    det,
    inv,
    eig,
    norm
)


np.set_printoptions(
    precision=10,
    suppress=True
)


# =====================================================
#
# Part 16
#
# Recovery Bass Compression Theorem
#
# =====================================================


n = 12
degree = 3
q = degree - 1


G = nx.random_regular_graph(
    degree,
    n,
    seed=42
)


print("\n===== Graph =====")
print("n =", n)
print("m =", G.number_of_edges())



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Directed edges
# -----------------------------------------------------


edges=[]

for a,b in G.edges():

    edges.append((a,b))
    edges.append((b,a))


E=len(edges)



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Hashimoto
# -----------------------------------------------------


B=np.zeros(
    (E,E)
)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(edges):

    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(edges):

        if b==c and d!=a:

            B[i,j]=1



print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")
print(B.shape)



# -----------------------------------------------------
# Adjacency
# -----------------------------------------------------


A=nx.to_numpy_array(G)


print("\n===== Adjacency =====")

print(A.shape)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Bass operator
#
# M(u)=I-uA+qu^2I
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


u=0.2


M=(
    np.eye(n)
    -
    u*A
    +
    q*u*u*np.eye(n)
)


RB=inv(M)



print("\n===== Recovery Bass kernel =====")

print(RB.shape)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Exact determinant theorem
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


Z_hash=1/det(
    np.eye(E)-u*B
)


Z_bass=(
(1-u*u)**(-(E-n))
/
det(M)
)



print("\n===== Bass identity =====")


print(
"Hashimoto"
)

print(Z_hash)


print(
"Bass"
)

print(Z_bass)


print(
"error"
)

print(
abs(Z_hash-Z_bass)
)



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# q recursive Green function
#
# w = 1/(lambda-qw)
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== q recursive Green =====")


for lam in eig(A)[0][:8]:

    roots=np.roots(
        [
            q,
            -lam,
            1
        ]
    )


    print(
        "lambda",
        lam
    )

    print(
        roots
    )



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Satake parameters
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Satake =====")


for lam in eig(A)[0][:8]:

    alpha,beta=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -lam,
            q
        ]
    )


    print(
        lam,
        "=>",
        alpha,
        beta
    )



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# Fredholm determinant
#
# L local factor
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== Fredholm local =====")


for lam in eig(A)[0][:5]:

    alpha,beta=np.roots(
        [
            1,
            -lam,
            q
        ]
    )


    local=(
        1/
        (
        (1-u*alpha)
        *
        (1-u*beta)
        )
    )


    print(
        local
    )



# -----------------------------------------------------
#
# stopping kernel
#
# -----------------------------------------------------


print("\n===== stopping kernel =====")


S=np.eye(n)-RB


sv=np.linalg.svd(S)[1]


print(
sv
)


print(
"dimension"
)

print(
np.sum(
sv<1e-10
)
)



print("""
=====================================

Part 16 Complete


Computed:

1. Exact Bass compression kernel

2. Ihara-Bass determinant identity

3. q>1 recursive Green function

4. Satake parameters

5. Fredholm local factor

6. Recovery stopping kernel


Next Part 17:

Recovery-Satake theorem

- spectral measure
- Euler product
- L-function extension

=====================================
""")

import numpy as np
import networkx as nx
from scipy.linalg import eigvals, det, inv, svd

np.set_printoptions(precision=10,suppress=True)

print("======================================")
print(" Recovery Bass Compression Part 17")
print("======================================")


# ============================================================
# 1. Graph construction
# ============================================================

G = nx.random_regular_graph(
    d=3,
    n=12,
    seed=42
)

nodes=list(G.nodes())
edges=list(G.edges())

n=len(nodes)
m=len(edges)

print("\n===== Graph =====")
print("vertices =",n)
print("edges    =",m)


# ============================================================
# 2. Directed edge space
# ============================================================

directed=[]

for a,b in edges:
    directed.append((a,b))
    directed.append((b,a))

E=len(directed)

edge_index={
    e:i for i,e in enumerate(directed)
}


# ============================================================
# 3. Hashimoto matrix
# ============================================================

B=np.zeros((E,E),dtype=complex)


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed):
    for j,(c,d) in enumerate(directed):

        if b==c and a!=d:
            B[i,j]=1


print("\n===== Hashimoto =====")
print(B.shape)


# ============================================================
# 4. Vertex adjacency
# ============================================================

A=nx.to_numpy_array(
    G,
    dtype=complex
)


print("\n===== Adjacency =====")
print(A.shape)



# ============================================================
# 5. Incidence compression
# ============================================================


C=np.zeros((n,E))
D=np.zeros((E,n))


for i,(a,b) in enumerate(directed):

    C[a,i]=1

    D[i,b]=1



print("\n===== Compression =====")
print("C",C.shape)
print("D",D.shape)



# ============================================================
# 6. Recovery vertex operator
# ============================================================


R_edge=B.copy()

# normalized edge operator
rho=max(abs(eigvals(B)))

R_edge=R_edge/rho


print("\n===== Edge Recovery =====")
print("rho =",rho)



# vertex compression

RA=C@R_edge@D


# normalization

RA=RA/np.linalg.norm(RA)


print("\n===== Vertex Recovery =====")
print(RA.shape)
print("norm =",np.linalg.norm(RA))



# ============================================================
# 7. Intertwining defect
# ============================================================


left=C@R_edge
right=RA@C


print("\n===== Intertwining =====")

print(
"||CR-RAC|| =",
np.linalg.norm(left-right)
)



# ============================================================
# 8. q>1 recursive Green kernel
# ============================================================


print("\n===== q recursion =====")


eigA=eigvals(A)


for lam in eigA[:8]:

    disc=np.sqrt(lam**2-4*(n-1))

    w1=(lam+disc)/(2*(n-1))
    w2=(lam-disc)/(2*(n-1))


    print(
        "lambda",
        lam,
        "=>",
        w1,
        w2
    )



# ============================================================
# 9. Recovery Green function
# ============================================================


z=0.2


GE=inv(
    np.eye(E)-z*R_edge
)


GV=inv(
    np.eye(n)-z*RA
)



print("\n===== Green =====")

print("edge")
print(GE.shape)

print("vertex")
print(GV.shape)



# ============================================================
# 10. Fredholm determinant
# ============================================================


ZE=det(
    np.eye(E)-z*R_edge
)


ZA=det(
    np.eye(n)-z*RA
)



print("\n===== Fredholm determinant =====")

print("edge determinant")
print(ZE)

print("vertex determinant")
print(ZA)



# ============================================================
# 11. Stopping kernel
# ============================================================


print("\n===== stopping kernel =====")


U,S,Vh=svd(
    np.eye(n)-RA
)


print("singular values")

print(S)



tol=1e-8

kernel_dim=np.sum(
    S<tol
)


print(
"kernel dimension",
kernel_dim
)



# ============================================================
# 12. Spectral symmetry
# ============================================================


print("\n===== Spectrum =====")

spec=eigvals(RA)

for x in spec:
    print(x)



# ============================================================
# 13. Satake reconstruction
# ============================================================


print("\n===== Satake =====")


for mu in eigvals(A)[:8]:

    disc=np.sqrt(mu**2-4)

    alpha=(mu+disc)/2
    beta=(mu-disc)/2

    print(
        mu,
        "=>",
        alpha,
        beta
    )
