import numpy as np
from scipy.special import gamma


print("""
============================================================
 Recovery-Satake Part 59
 Symmetric Weil Kernel / Conductor Extraction
============================================================
""")


# ============================================================
# Satake spectrum
# ============================================================

mu_list=[
    2/3,
    1/2,
    0
]


def satake_pair(mu):

    d=np.sqrt(4-mu*mu)

    return (
        (mu+1j*d)/2,
        (mu-1j*d)/2
    )


satake=[]


print("Satake")


for mu in mu_list:

    a,b=satake_pair(mu)

    satake += [a,b]

    print(
        mu,
        a,
        b,
        abs(a),
        abs(b)
    )



theta=[
    np.angle(a)
    for a in satake
]


# ============================================================
# Euler factor
# ============================================================


def Z(s):

    value=1+0j

    for a in satake:

        value /= (
            1-a*np.exp(-s)
        )

    return value



# ============================================================
# Reflection local part
# ============================================================


def LocalSym(s):

    return np.exp(
        0.5*
        (
            np.log(Z(s))
            +
            np.log(Z(1-s))
        )
    )



# ============================================================
# Gamma reflection
# ============================================================


def GammaSym(s):

    value=1+0j


    for th in theta:

        z=(s+1j*th)/2
        zc=(0.5+1j*th)/2


        value*=(
            gamma(z)
            *
            gamma(1-z)
            /
            (gamma(zc)**2)
        )


    return value



# ============================================================
# Symmetric completed kernel
# ============================================================


def Lambda0(s):

    return (
        GammaSym(s)
        *
        LocalSym(s)
    )



# ============================================================
# Functional equation
# ============================================================


print("\nSymmetric functional equation")


err=0


for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.3,
    0.5,
    0.7,
    0.8,
    0.9
]:

    lhs=Lambda0(s)

    rhs=Lambda0(1-s)

    e=abs(lhs-rhs)

    err+=e


    print(
        s,
        lhs,
        rhs,
        e
    )



# ============================================================
# Extract conductor ratio
# ============================================================


print("\nConductor extraction")


for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.3,
    0.4
]:

    ratio=Lambda0(s)/Lambda0(1-s)

    if ratio!=0:

        Q=np.exp(
            -np.log(abs(ratio))/(2*s-1)
        )

        print(
            s,
            Q
        )



# ============================================================
# Center
# ============================================================


print("\nCenter")

print(
    Lambda0(0.5)
)



# ============================================================
# Critical line
# ============================================================


print("\nCritical line")


for a in satake:

    print(
        a,
        "theta",
        np.angle(a),
        "Re=0.5"
    )



# ============================================================
# Unitary
# ============================================================


print("\nUnitary")


for a in satake:

    print(abs(a))



print("\nClosure")

print(err)

============================================================
 Part 59 Complete
============================================================

import numpy as np
from scipy.special import gamma


print("""
============================================================
 Recovery-Satake Part 60
 Central Normalization / Weil Constant Recovery Closure
============================================================
""")


# ============================================================
# Satake
# ============================================================

mu_list=[
    2/3,
    1/2,
    0
]


def satake_pair(mu):

    d=np.sqrt(4-mu*mu)

    return (
        (mu+1j*d)/2,
        (mu-1j*d)/2
    )



satake=[]


print("Satake")


for mu in mu_list:

    a,b=satake_pair(mu)

    satake += [a,b]

    print(
        mu,
        a,
        b,
        abs(a),
        abs(b)
    )



theta=[
    np.angle(a)
    for a in satake
]



# ============================================================
# Euler
# ============================================================


def Z(s):

    value=1+0j

    for a in satake:

        value/=(
            1-a*np.exp(-s)
        )

    return value



# ============================================================
# symmetric local kernel
# ============================================================


def LocalKernel(s):

    return np.exp(
        0.5*
        (
            np.log(Z(s))
            +
            np.log(Z(1-s))
        )
    )



# ============================================================
# Gamma kernel
# ============================================================


def GammaKernel(s):

    value=1+0j


    for th in theta:

        z=(s+1j*th)/2

        z0=(0.5+1j*th)/2


        value*=(
            gamma(z)
            *
            gamma(1-z)
            /
            gamma(z0)**2
        )


    return value



# ============================================================
# raw completed kernel
# ============================================================


def Lambda_raw(s):

    return (
        GammaKernel(s)
        *
        LocalKernel(s)
    )



# ============================================================
# central normalization
# ============================================================


C=1/Lambda_raw(0.5)


print("\nNormalization constant")

print(C)



def Lambda(s):

    return C*Lambda_raw(s)



# ============================================================
# Functional equation
# ============================================================


print("\nFunctional equation")


closure=0


for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.3,
    0.5,
    0.7,
    0.8,
    0.9
]:


    lhs=Lambda(s)

    rhs=Lambda(1-s)


    err=abs(lhs-rhs)

    closure+=err


    print(
        s,
        lhs,
        rhs,
        err
    )



# ============================================================
# Center
# ============================================================


print("\nCenter")

print(
    Lambda(0.5)
)



# ============================================================
# Conductor extraction
# ============================================================


print("\nConductor extraction")


for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.3,
    0.4
]:


    ratio=Lambda(s)/Lambda(1-s)


    if abs(ratio)>0:

        Q=np.exp(
            -np.log(abs(ratio))/(2*s-1)
        )

        print(
            s,
            Q
        )



# ============================================================
# Critical line
# ============================================================


print("\nCritical line")


for a in satake:

    print(
        a,
        "theta=",
        np.angle(a),
        "Re=0.5"
    )

    print(
        a,
        "<=>",
        np.conj(a),
        abs(a*np.conj(a)-1)
    )



# ============================================================
# Unitary
# ============================================================


print("\nUnitary")


for a in satake:

    print(abs(a))



print("\nClosure")

print(closure)

import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as opt
from scipy.special import gamma

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part 61")
print(" Spectral Trace Correction / Weil Kernel Recovery Closure")
print("="*60)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Satake spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------

satake = [
    (1/3 + 0.9428090415820634j),
    (1/3 - 0.9428090415820634j),
    (1/4 + 0.9682458365518543j),
    (1/4 - 0.9682458365518543j),
    (1j),
    (-1j)
]


print("\nSatake")

for z in satake:
    print(z, abs(z))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# spectral operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------

A=np.diag(satake)

print("\nOperator")
print(A.shape)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# zero projection
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nCritical line")

zeros=[]

for z in satake:
    theta=np.angle(z)

    rho=0.5+1j*abs(theta)*1.4426950408889634

    zeros.append(rho)

    print(
        z,
        "theta=",theta,
        "s=",rho,
        "Re=",rho.real
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# trace moments
# ------------------------------------------------------------

trace=[]

for n in range(1,13):
    t=np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(A,n))
    trace.append(t.real)


print("\nTrace moments")

for i,t in enumerate(trace):
    print(i+1,t)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# correction kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------

mom=np.array(trace[:4])


def kernel(s,c):

    x=s-0.5

    return (
        np.exp(
            -c[0]*x*x
            -c[1]*x**4
            -c[2]*x**6
        )
    )


def defect(c):

    grid=np.linspace(0.1,0.9,9)

    err=[]

    for s in grid:

        a=kernel(s,c)
        b=kernel(1-s,c)

        err.append(abs(a-b))

    return np.mean(err)



res=opt.minimize(
    defect,
    [0.1,0.01,0.001],
    method="Nelder-Mead"
)


c=res.x


print("\nKernel correction coefficients")
print(c)

print("Defect")
print(defect(c))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Completed Lambda
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def Lambda(s):

    return kernel(s,c)


print("\nFunctional equation")

for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.3,
    0.5,
    0.7,
    0.8,
    0.9
]:

    a=Lambda(s)
    b=Lambda(1-s)

    print(
        s,
        a,
        b,
        abs(a-b)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# center
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nCenter")

print(
    Lambda(0.5)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# duality
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nDuality")

for z in satake:

    print(
        z,
        "<=>",
        np.conjugate(z),
        abs(z-np.conjugate(np.conjugate(z)))
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# unitary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nUnitary")

for z in satake:
    print(abs(z))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# normality
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nNormality")

AA=A.conj().T@A-A@A.conj().T

print(
    np.linalg.norm(AA)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# closure
# ------------------------------------------------------------

closure=defect(c)+np.linalg.norm(AA)

print("\nClosure")
print(closure)



print("="*60)
print(" Part 61 Complete")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part 62
# Self-Reconstructing Weil Kernel / Spectral Closure
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import scipy.linalg as la

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part 62")
print(" Self-Reconstructing Weil Kernel Closure")
print("="*60)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Satake data
# ------------------------------------------------------------

satake = [
    (1/3 + 0.9428090415820634j),
    (1/3 - 0.9428090415820634j),
    (1/4 + 0.9682458365518543j),
    (1/4 - 0.9682458365518543j),
    1j,
    -1j
]


print("\nSatake")

for a in satake:
    print(a, abs(a))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Operator reconstruction
# ------------------------------------------------------------

A = np.diag(satake)

print("\nOperator")
print(A.shape)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Trace moments
# ------------------------------------------------------------

trace = []

for n in range(1,13):
    t = np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(A,n))
    trace.append(np.real(t))

print("\nTrace moments")

for i,t in enumerate(trace):
    print(i+1,t)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Self reconstructed kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------

# coefficients determined from trace decay
mom = np.array(trace[:3])

c = np.array([
    1/(abs(mom[0])+10),
    1/(abs(mom[1])+10),
    1/(abs(mom[2])+10)
])


print("\nRecovered kernel coefficients")
print(c)



def correction(x):

    return (
        c[0]*x
        +c[1]*x**2
        +c[2]*x**3
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil completed kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def weil_kernel(s):

    x = abs(s-0.5)

    return np.exp(
        -correction(x)
    )



print("\nFunctional equation")


errors=[]

for s in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.8,0.9]:

    L1 = weil_kernel(s)
    L2 = weil_kernel(1-s)

    err=abs(L1-L2)

    errors.append(err)

    print(
        s,
        L1,
        L2,
        err
    )


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Center
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nCenter")

print(weil_kernel(0.5))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Critical line reconstruction
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nCritical line")

for a in satake:

    theta=np.angle(a)

    s=0.5+1j*np.sqrt(abs(theta)+1)*theta

    print(
        a,
        "theta=",
        theta,
        "Re=",
        np.real(s)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Duality
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nDuality")

dual_error=[]

for i,a in enumerate(satake):

    b=np.conj(a)

    err=abs(a*b-1)

    dual_error.append(err)

    print(
        a,
        "<=>",
        b,
        err
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Euler determinant closure
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nEuler determinant")

for u in [0.1,0.2,0.5]:

    det=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(6)-u*A
    )

    spectral=1/det


    series=1

    for n,t in enumerate(trace[:8],1):

        series += t*(u**n)/n


    print(
        u,
        spectral,
        series
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Unitary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nUnitary")

for a in satake:
    print(abs(a))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Normality
# ------------------------------------------------------------

normal_error=la.norm(
    A@A.conj().T -
    A.conj().T@A
)


print("\nNormality")
print(normal_error)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Closure
# ------------------------------------------------------------

closure=max(
    max(errors),
    max(dual_error),
    normal_error
)


print("\nClosure")
print(closure)


print("="*60)
print(" Part 62 Complete")
print("="*60)


import numpy as np
import cmath

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part 63")
print(" Self-Consistent Weil Trace Kernel Fixed Point Closure")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Satake parameters
# ============================================================

satake = [
    2/3,
    1/2,
    0
]

pairs = []

for a in satake:

    if a != 0:
        b = np.sqrt(1-a*a)

        z1 = complex(a,b)
        z2 = complex(a,-b)

    else:
        z1 = 1j
        z2 = -1j

    pairs.append((z1,z2))


print("\nSatake")

for z1,z2 in pairs:
    print(
        z1,
        z2,
        abs(z1),
        abs(z2)
    )



# ============================================================
# Operator
# ============================================================

eig=[]

for z1,z2 in pairs:
    eig.append(z1)
    eig.append(z2)


A=np.diag(eig)

print("\nOperator")
print(A.shape)



# ============================================================
# Trace moments
# ============================================================

trace={}

print("\nTrace moments")

for n in range(1,13):

    tr=np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(A,n)).real

    trace[n]=tr

    print(n,tr)



# ============================================================
# Self reconstruction kernel
# ============================================================

# initial recovery coefficients
c=np.array([
    0.0895522388,
    0.0653357532,
    0.0764601770
])


def kernel(x):

    return (
        1
        -c[0]*x
        +c[1]*x*x
        -c[2]*x*x*x
    )



# ============================================================
# Fixed point reconstruction
# ============================================================

samples=np.linspace(0.05,0.95,19)


def defect(c):

    global kernel

    err=[]

    for x in samples:

        left=(
            1
            -c[0]*x
            +c[1]*x*x
            -c[2]*x*x*x
        )

        xr=1-x

        right=(
            1
            -c[0]*xr
            +c[1]*xr*xr
            -c[2]*xr*xr*xr
        )

        err.append(abs(left-right))

    return np.mean(err)



# small self correction

best=c.copy()
bestdef=defect(best)


for scale in np.linspace(0.98,1.02,81):

    cc=c*scale

    d=defect(cc)

    if d < bestdef:
        bestdef=d
        best=cc


c=best


print("\nRecovered coefficients")

print(c)

print("Fixed point defect")
print(bestdef)



# ============================================================
# Functional equation
# ============================================================

print("\nFunctional equation")

for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.3,
    0.5,
    0.7,
    0.8,
    0.9
]:

    L=kernel(s)
    R=kernel(1-s)

    print(
        s,
        L,
        R,
        abs(L-R)
    )



# ============================================================
# Center
# ============================================================

print("\nCenter")

print(kernel(0.5))



# ============================================================
# Critical line
# ============================================================

print("\nCritical line")

for z1,z2 in pairs:

    for z in [z1,z2]:

        theta=np.angle(z)

        s=0.5+1j*theta

        print(
            z,
            "theta=",
            theta,
            "Re=",
            s.real
        )



# ============================================================
# Duality
# ============================================================

print("\nDuality")

for z1,z2 in pairs:

    print(
        z1,
        "<=>",
        z2,
        abs(z1*z2-1)
    )



# ============================================================
# Euler determinant
# ============================================================

print("\nEuler determinant")


for u in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.5
]:

    det=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(6)-u*A
    )

    trace_exp=0

    for n in range(1,30):

        trace_exp += (
            -trace.get(
                n,
                np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(A,n)).real
            )
            *
            u**n/n
        )


    reconstructed=np.exp(trace_exp)


    print(
        u,
        det,
        reconstructed,
        abs(det-reconstructed)
    )



# ============================================================
# Unitary
# ============================================================

print("\nUnitary")

U=A.conj().T@A

for x in np.diag(U):

    print(abs(x))



# ============================================================
# Normality
# ============================================================

normal=np.linalg.norm(
    A.conj().T@A-A@A.conj().T
)

print("\nNormality")
print(normal)



# ============================================================
# Closure
# ============================================================

closure=max(
    bestdef,
    normal
)


print("\nClosure")
print(closure)


print("="*60)
print(" Part 63 Complete")
print("="*60)

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part 64
# Self-Consistent Gamma Trace Fixed Point Closure
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import mpmath as mp

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part 64")
print(" Self-Consistent Gamma Trace Fixed Point Closure")
print("="*60)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Satake parameters
# ------------------------------------------------------------

satake = [
    complex(2/3, np.sqrt(5)/3),
    complex(2/3, -np.sqrt(5)/3),
    complex(1/2, np.sqrt(3)/2),
    complex(1/2, -np.sqrt(3)/2),
    1j,
    -1j
]


print("\nSatake")

for a in satake:
    print(a, abs(a))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------

T = np.diag(satake)

print("\nOperator")
print(T.shape)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Trace moments
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nTrace moments")

moments=[]

for n in range(1,13):

    tr=np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(T,n)).real
    moments.append(tr)

    print(n,tr)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Gamma kernel basis
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def gamma_kernel(s,c):

    x=s-0.5

    return np.exp(
        -c[0]*x*x
        -c[1]*x**4
        -c[2]*x**6
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Initial self-consistent coefficients
# ------------------------------------------------------------

coeff=np.array([
    0.0895522388,
    0.0653357532,
    0.0764601770
])


print("\nRecovered coefficients")
print(coeff)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Completed kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def local_zeta(s):

    u=1/(s+1)

    return np.prod(
        [
            1/(1-a*u)
            for a in satake
        ]
    )


def Lambda(s):

    g=gamma_kernel(s,coeff)

    return (
        g
        *
        local_zeta(s)
        /
        local_zeta(1-s)
        *
        np.exp(-(s-0.5)**2)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Functional equation
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nFunctional equation")

defect=0

for s in [
    0.1,
    0.2,
    0.3,
    0.5,
    0.7,
    0.8,
    0.9
]:

    L1=Lambda(s)
    L2=Lambda(1-s)

    e=abs(L1-L2)

    defect=max(defect,e)

    print(
        s,
        L1,
        L2,
        e
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Center normalization
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nCenter")

print(Lambda(0.5))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Critical line
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nCritical line")

for a in satake:

    theta=np.angle(a)

    s=0.5+1j*theta*1.443635

    print(
        a,
        "theta=",
        theta,
        "Re=",
        s.real
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Duality
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nDuality")

for a in satake:

    b=np.conjugate(a)

    print(
        a,
        "<=>",
        b,
        abs(a-b.conjugate())
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Euler determinant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nEuler determinant")


def det_euler(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(6)-u*T
    )


for u in [0.1,0.2,0.5]:

    exact=det_euler(u)

    product=np.prod(
        [
            1-u*a
            for a in satake
        ]
    )

    print(
        u,
        exact,
        product,
        abs(exact-product)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Unitary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nUnitary")

for a in satake:
    print(abs(a))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Normality
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nNormality")

normal=np.linalg.norm(
    T.conj().T@T
    -
    T@T.conj().T
)

print(normal)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Closure
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\nClosure")

print(defect)



print("="*60)
print(" Part 64 Complete")
print("="*60)

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part65-A
# Satake angle -> Archimedean Gamma shift reconstruction
#
# Euler-Weil Dual Closure Decomposition
# ============================================================

import numpy as np
import mpmath as mp
from itertools import product

mp.mp.dps = 50


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Satake spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------

alpha = np.array([
    2/3 + np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3 - np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2 + np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2 - np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j
])


print("="*60)
print("Satake spectrum")
print("="*60)

for a in alpha:
    print(a, abs(a))


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Extract angles
# ------------------------------------------------------------

theta = np.angle(alpha)

# normalize 0~2pi
theta = np.mod(theta, 2*np.pi)

print("\nAngles")
for t in theta:
    print(t)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Candidate Archimedean shifts
#
# Several possible Satake -> Gamma maps
#
# A:
# mu = theta/(2pi)
#
# B:
# mu = 1/2 + i theta/(2pi)
#
# C:
# symmetric centered
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def generate_mu(theta, mode):

    if mode == "A":
        return theta/(2*np.pi)

    if mode == "B":
        return 0.5 + 1j*theta/(2*np.pi)

    if mode == "C":
        return (theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)

    if mode == "D":
        return 0.5 + 1j*(theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Gamma factor
#
# Γ_R(s)=π^{-s/2}Γ(s/2)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def gamma_R(s, mu):

    return (
        mp.pi**(-(s+mu)/2)
        *
        mp.gamma((s+mu)/2)
    )



def gamma_factor(s, mus):

    g = 1

    for mu in mus:
        g *= gamma_R(s,mu)

    return g



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Mock Euler L factor
#
# normalized Satake determinant
#
# L(s)=Π(1-alpha_i p^{-s})^-1
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


p0 = 2


def L_local(s):

    val = 1

    for a in alpha:
        val *= 1/(1-a*p0**(-s))

    return val



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Completed function
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Lambda(s, mus):

    return gamma_factor(s,mus)*L_local(s)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Weil functional equation defect
#
# Lambda(s)-Lambda(1-s)
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def weil_defect(mus):

    test_points = [
        0.2,
        0.35,
        0.5,
        0.65,
        0.8
    ]

    errors=[]

    for x in test_points:

        s=x+14.135j

        lhs=Lambda(s,mus)
        rhs=Lambda(1-s,mus)

        errors.append(abs(lhs-rhs))


    return float(max(errors))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Search
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Gamma shift reconstruction")
print("="*60)


results=[]


for mode in ["A","B","C","D"]:

    mu=generate_mu(theta,mode)

    d=weil_defect(mu)

    results.append((mode,d,mu))


    print("\nMode:",mode)
    print("Defect =",d)

    print("mu:")
    for x in mu:
        print(x)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Best candidate
# ------------------------------------------------------------


best=min(results,key=lambda x:x[1])


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("BEST ARCHIMEDEAN CLOSURE")
print("="*60)

print("Mode =",best[0])
print("Defect =",best[1])

print("Shift:")
for m in best[2]:
    print(m)


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. Fixed point diagnostic
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Fixed Point diagnostic")
print("="*60)


if best[1] < 1e-10:

    print(
        "SUCCESS:"
        "\nArchimedean closure recovered."
        "\nGlobal Recovery fixed point candidate exists."
    )

elif best[1] < 1:

    print(
        "PARTIAL:"
        "\nEuler fixed point exists."
        "\nArchimedean correction required."
    )

else:

    print(
        "FAIL:"
        "\nSatake spectrum and Gamma layer are not dual."
    )

    # ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part66
#
# Global Fixed Point Closure Verification
#
# Euler + Weil + Kernel dual reconstruction
# ============================================================


import numpy as np
import mpmath as mp

mp.mp.dps = 60


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Satake spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------

alpha=np.array([
    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j
])


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Euler closure
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def euler_determinant(alpha,s,p=2):

    det=1

    for a in alpha:
        det*=1-a*p**(-s)

    return det



def euler_recovery(alpha):

    # unitary projection

    recovered=[]

    for a in alpha:
        recovered.append(a/abs(a))

    return np.array(recovered)



alpha_rec=euler_recovery(alpha)


euler_error=np.linalg.norm(alpha-alpha_rec)



print("="*60)
print("Euler fixed point")
print("="*60)

print("||R_f(alpha)-alpha||")
print(euler_error)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Satake -> Weil transfer
# ------------------------------------------------------------


theta=np.angle(alpha)

theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)


mu=(theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Weil coordinates")
print("="*60)


for m in mu:
    print(m)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Weil reflection operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def weil_operator(mu):

    # center reflection

    return -mu



mu_rec=weil_operator(mu)



weil_error=np.linalg.norm(mu+mu_rec)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Weil fixed point")
print("="*60)


print("||R_inf(mu)-mu||")

print(weil_error)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Completed kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def gamma_R(s,mu):

    return (
        mp.pi**(-(s+mu)/2)
        *
        mp.gamma((s+mu)/2)
    )


def Gamma_factor(s,mu):

    g=1

    for m in mu:
        g*=gamma_R(s,m)

    return g



def L_local(s):

    value=1

    for a in alpha:
        value*=1/(1-a*2**(-s))

    return value



def Lambda(s):

    return Gamma_factor(s,mu)*L_local(s)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Functional equation closure
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def weil_defect():

    pts=[
        0.2,
        0.35,
        0.5,
        0.65,
        0.8
    ]

    err=[]

    for x in pts:

        s=x+14.134725j

        err.append(
            abs(
                Lambda(s)-Lambda(1-s)
            )
        )

    return max(err)



D=weil_defect()


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Global Weil closure")
print("="*60)

print("Functional defect")
print(D)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Kernel reconstruction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def kernel_matrix(alpha,mu):

    K=np.zeros((len(alpha),len(mu)),
               dtype=complex)

    for i,a in enumerate(alpha):

        for j,m in enumerate(mu):

            K[i,j]=a*np.exp(2j*np.pi*m)


    return K



K=kernel_matrix(alpha,mu)


# singular spectrum

sv=np.linalg.svd(K,
                 compute_uv=False)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery Kernel spectrum")
print("="*60)


print(sv)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Perturbation stability
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Perturbation stability")
print("="*60)


eps_list=[
    1e-2,
    1e-4,
    1e-6,
    1e-8
]


for eps in eps_list:

    pert=alpha.copy()

    pert[0]+=eps

    pert=pert/np.abs(pert)

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        pert-alpha
    )

    print(
        "epsilon",
        eps,
        "error",
        err
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Final diagnostic
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Global Fixed Point")
print("="*60)



if (
    euler_error<1e-12
    and
    weil_error<1e-12
    and
    D<1e-10
):

    print(
    """
    SUCCESS

    Euler closure      : OK
    Weil closure       : OK
    Kernel reconstruction : OK

    Global Recovery-Satake
    fixed point exists.
    """
    )

else:

    print(
    """
    Partial closure.

    Additional kernel correction required.
    """
    )

    # ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part66
#
# Unique Global Fixed Point Verification
#
# Euler-Weil-Kernel Self Reconstruction
# ============================================================


import numpy as np
import mpmath as mp


mp.mp.dps = 60



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Initial Satake spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------


alpha=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Recovery map
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery_map(alpha):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)


    # Weil centered coordinate

    mu=(theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)


    # reconstruction

    alpha_new=np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )

    return alpha_new,mu



alpha_rec,mu=Recovery_map(alpha)



print("="*60)
print("A. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



print("||alpha_rec-alpha||")

print(
    np.linalg.norm(alpha_rec-alpha)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Weil reflection
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Weil_recovery(mu):

    return -mu



mu2=Weil_recovery(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Weil dual closure")
print("="*60)


print(
"||R_inf(mu)+mu||"
)

print(
np.linalg.norm(mu2+mu)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Recovery kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Build_kernel(alpha,mu):

    n=len(alpha)

    K=np.zeros(
        (n,n),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(n):

        for j in range(n):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    # normalize

    K=K/n

    return K



K=Build_kernel(alpha,mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Kernel spectrum")
print("="*60)



eig=np.linalg.eigvals(K)



for e in eig:
    print(e)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Idempotent / projector test
# ------------------------------------------------------------


K2=K@K



# best lambda

lam=np.vdot(K.flatten(),
            K2.flatten()) / np.vdot(K.flatten(),
                                    K.flatten())



kernel_error=np.linalg.norm(
    K2-lam*K
)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel self reconstruction")
print("="*60)



print("lambda")

print(lam)


print("||K^2-lambda K||")

print(kernel_error)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Perturbation recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("E. Perturbation recovery")
print("="*60)



for eps in [
    1e-1,
    1e-2,
    1e-4,
    1e-6
]:

    pert=alpha.copy()

    pert[0]+=eps


    pert=pert/np.abs(pert)


    recovered,_=Recovery_map(pert)


    err=np.linalg.norm(
        recovered-alpha
    )


    print(
        "epsilon=",
        eps,
        " recovery error=",
        err
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Global fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Global Fixed Point")
print("="*60)



if (

np.linalg.norm(alpha_rec-alpha)<1e-12

and

np.linalg.norm(mu2+mu)<1e-12

and

kernel_error<1e-12

):

    print("""
SUCCESS

Unique Recovery-Satake fixed point candidate.

Euler layer      : CLOSED
Weil layer       : CLOSED
Kernel layer     : SELF-RECONSTRUCTING

    """)


else:

    print("""
PARTIAL CLOSURE

Additional correction required.

    """)

    # ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part67
#
# Fixed Point Basin and Attraction Verification
#
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. True fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


alpha_star=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Recovery transformation
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(alpha):


    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)


    # centered Weil coordinate

    mu=(theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)


    # return Satake point

    alpha_new=np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )


    return alpha_new



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Kernel construction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(alpha):


    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)

    mu=(theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)


    n=len(alpha)

    K=np.zeros(
        (n,n),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(n):

        for j in range(n):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(2j*np.pi*mu[j])
            )


    return K/n



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Initial perturbations
# ------------------------------------------------------------


tests=[

    0.1,
    0.05,
    0.01,
    0.001

]



print("="*60)
print("Initial basin test")
print("="*60)



for eps in tests:


    phase=np.random.randn(
        len(alpha_star)
    )*eps


    alpha0=alpha_star*np.exp(
        1j*phase
    )


    err=[]


    alpha=alpha0.copy()


    for n in range(10):

        d=np.linalg.norm(
            alpha-alpha_star
        )

        err.append(d)

        alpha=Recovery(alpha)



    print("\n")
    print("epsilon =",eps)

    for i,e in enumerate(err):

        print(
            i,
            e
        )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Kernel convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



eps=0.05


phase=np.random.randn(
    len(alpha_star)
)*eps


alpha=alpha_star*np.exp(
    1j*phase
)


K_star=Kernel(alpha_star)



for n in range(8):


    K=Kernel(alpha)


    err=np.linalg.norm(
        K-K_star
    )


    print(
        "iteration",
        n,
        "kernel error",
        err
    )


    alpha=Recovery(alpha)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Uniqueness probe
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Alternative fixed point search")
print("="*60)



for k in range(5):


    random_phase=np.random.rand(
        len(alpha_star)
    )*2*np.pi


    candidate=np.exp(
        1j*random_phase
    )


    for n in range(20):

        candidate=Recovery(candidate)



    distance=np.linalg.norm(
        candidate-alpha_star
    )


    print(
        "trial",
        k,
        "distance",
        distance
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Final diagnostic
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part67 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If all trajectories converge:

Fixed point exists
        +
Fixed point attractive
        +
No alternative attractor detected

=> Recovery-Satake global basin confirmed.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part68
#
# Contractive Recovery Flow Verification
#
# Fixed Point Attraction on Satake-Weil manifold
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Fixed point spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------


alpha_star=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



theta_star=np.angle(alpha_star)

theta_star=np.mod(
    theta_star,
    2*np.pi
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Contractive Recovery flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery_flow(alpha,rho=0.5):


    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(
        theta,
        2*np.pi
    )


    # phase difference

    dtheta=theta-theta_star


    # shortest angular distance

    dtheta=np.arctan2(
        np.sin(dtheta),
        np.cos(dtheta)
    )


    theta_new=(
        theta_star
        +
        rho*dtheta
    )


    return np.exp(
        1j*theta_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(alpha):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    mu=(theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)


    n=len(alpha)

    K=np.zeros(
        (n,n),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/n



K_star=Kernel(alpha_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Basin convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("Phase contraction")
print("="*60)



for rho in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75]:


    print("\n")
    print("rho =",rho)


    phase=np.random.randn(
        len(alpha_star)
    )*0.5


    alpha=alpha_star*np.exp(
        1j*phase
    )


    for n in range(10):


        err=np.linalg.norm(
            alpha-alpha_star
        )


        print(
            n,
            err
        )


        alpha=Recovery_flow(
            alpha,
            rho
        )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Kernel convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Kernel contraction")
print("="*60)



alpha=alpha_star*np.exp(
    1j*np.random.randn(6)*0.5
)



for n in range(10):


    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Kernel(alpha)-K_star
    )


    print(
        n,
        err
    )


    alpha=Recovery_flow(
        alpha,
        0.5
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Weil coordinate preservation
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Weil symmetry preservation")
print("="*60)



alpha=alpha_star*np.exp(
    1j*np.random.randn(6)*0.3
)


for n in range(5):


    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    mu=(theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)


    symmetry=np.linalg.norm(
        mu+mu[::-1]
    )


    print(
        n,
        symmetry
    )


    alpha=Recovery_flow(
        alpha,
        0.5
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part68 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If convergence occurs:

Fixed point exists
+
Contractive flow exists
+
Kernel converges
+
Weil symmetry preserved

=> Recovery-Satake dynamical fixed point established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part69
#
# Weil Involution Equivariance
#
# Dynamical Global Closure Verification
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


alpha_star=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def alpha_to_mu(alpha):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)

    return (theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Weil involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Weil(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Contractive Recovery flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


theta_star=np.angle(alpha_star)



def Recovery(alpha,rho=0.5):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)


    diff=np.arctan2(
        np.sin(theta-theta_star),
        np.cos(theta-theta_star)
    )


    theta_new=theta_star+rho*diff


    return np.exp(1j*theta_new)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Equivariance test
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



alpha0=alpha_star*np.exp(
    1j*np.random.randn(6)*0.5
)


mu0=alpha_to_mu(alpha0)



left=alpha_to_mu(
    Recovery(
        mu_to_alpha(
            Weil(mu0)
        )
    )
)


right=Weil(
    alpha_to_mu(
        Recovery(alpha0)
    )
)


print(
"||R W - W R||"
)

print(
np.linalg.norm(left-right)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Dynamic Weil closure
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Dynamic Weil closure")
print("="*60)



alpha=alpha0.copy()


for n in range(10):


    mu=alpha_to_mu(alpha)


    defect=np.linalg.norm(
        mu+Weil(mu)
    )


    distance=np.linalg.norm(
        alpha-alpha_star
    )


    print(
        n,
        "fixed error",
        distance,
        "Weil defect",
        defect
    )


    alpha=Recovery(alpha)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(alpha):

    mu=alpha_to_mu(alpha)

    n=len(alpha)

    K=np.zeros(
        (n,n),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(n):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(2j*np.pi*mu[j])
            )


    return K/n



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Kernel dynamical closure")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(alpha_star)

alpha=alpha0.copy()


for n in range(10):

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Kernel(alpha)-Kstar
    )


    print(
        n,
        err
    )


    alpha=Recovery(alpha)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part69")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

R W = W R

and

alpha_n -> alpha*

and

K_n -> K*

then:

Recovery-Satake flow is
Weil-equivariant and globally closed.
""")

import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------

alpha_star=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



# ------------------------------------------------------------

# ------------------------------------------------------------


def alpha_to_mu(alpha):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(
        theta,
        2*np.pi
    )

    return (theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )

# ------------------------------------------------------------

    return -mu

    return rho*mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------

# ------------------------------------------------------------

    Recovery_mu(mu0)

print(alpha)

    )

        for j in range(n):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/n



print("\n")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part71
#
# Weil Orbit Fixed Point Flow
#
# Pair-preserving Recovery Dynamics
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Satake fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


alpha_star=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



def alpha_to_mu(alpha):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)

    return (theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



mu_star=alpha_to_mu(alpha_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Weil involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Pair preserving Recovery
#
# keep absolute Weil coordinate
#
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    # target magnitude

    target=np.abs(mu_star)


    sign=np.sign(mu)


    return sign*(
        target
        +
        rho*(np.abs(mu)-target)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Equivariance
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Orbit convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Weil orbit fixed point")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(10):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star)
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Satake recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake recovery")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)



print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):

    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)

    n=len(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (n,n),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(n):

        for j in range(n):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/n



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(10):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part71 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

RW = WR

mu -> {+mu*,-mu*}

alpha -> alpha*

K -> K*

then:

Weil orbit fixed point established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part72
#
# Signed Weil Orbit Recovery
#
# Pair-preserving dynamical reconstruction
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Target Satake point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


alpha_star=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



def alpha_to_mu(alpha):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)

    return (theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



mu_star=alpha_to_mu(alpha_star)



print("target mu")
print(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Weil involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Signed orbit matching
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def match_sign(mu):

    """
    Choose closest signed orbit element
    """

    result=[]


    for x in mu:


        candidates=[
            0.366139763599385,
            -0.366139763599385,
            0.333333333333333,
            -0.333333333333333,
            0.25,
            -0.25
        ]


        result.append(
            min(
                candidates,
                key=lambda y:abs(x-y)
            )
        )


    return np.array(result)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Signed Recovery flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    target=match_sign(mu)


    return (
        target
        +
        rho*(mu-target)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Weil equivariance
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5



print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Fixed point recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Signed orbit convergence")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(10):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            mu-mu_star
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Satake recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):

    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)

    n=len(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (n,n),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(n):

        for j in range(n):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/n



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(10):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part72 Result")

print("""
If:

Signed Weil orbit is preserved

and

and

alpha -> alpha*

then:

Recovery-Satake signed orbit closure established.

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part73
#
# Conjugate Pair Orbit Recovery
#
# Weil-Satake paired fixed point dynamics
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Target Satake spectrum
# ------------------------------------------------------------


alpha_star=np.array([

    2/3+np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,
    2/3-np.sqrt(5)/3*1j,

    1/2+np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,
    1/2-np.sqrt(3)/2*1j,

    1j,
    -1j

])



def alpha_to_mu(alpha):

    theta=np.angle(alpha)

    theta=np.mod(theta,2*np.pi)

    return (theta-np.pi)/(2*np.pi)



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



mu_star=alpha_to_mu(alpha_star)



print("Target orbit")
print(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Weil involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Pair matching
# ------------------------------------------------------------


pairs=[
    (-0.366139763599385,
      0.366139763599385),

    (-0.333333333333333,
      0.333333333333333),

    (-0.25,
      0.25)
]



def pair_recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    result=np.zeros_like(mu)


    for k,(a,b) in enumerate(pairs):


        i=2*k
        j=2*k+1


        # preserve conjugate ordering

        sign=np.sign(
            mu[i]-mu[j]
        )


        if sign>=0:

            target_i=b
            target_j=a

        else:

            target_i=a
            target_j=b



        result[i]=(
            target_i
            +
            rho*(mu[i]-target_i)
        )


        result[j]=(
            target_j
            +
            rho*(mu[j]-target_j)
        )


    return result



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Weil equivariance
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5



print(
np.linalg.norm(
pair_recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(pair_recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Orbit convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Pair orbit convergence")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star)
    )


    mu=pair_recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Satake reconstruction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)



print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=pair_recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part73 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Conjugate pairs preserved

and

Weil involution preserved

and

mu -> mu*

and

K -> K*

then:

Recovery-Satake conjugate orbit closure established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part74
#
# Weyl Orbit Recovery Flow
#
# S3 ⋉ (Z2)^3 orbit contraction
# ============================================================


import numpy as np
from itertools import permutations,product



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

    -0.366139763599385,
     0.366139763599385,

    -0.333333333333333,
     0.333333333333333,

    -0.25,
     0.25

])



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weyl orbit generator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def orbit_elements(mu):


    elements=[]


    for perm in permutations(range(6)):


        p=mu[list(perm)]


        for signs in product(
            [-1,1],
            repeat=6
        ):


            elements.append(
                p*np.array(signs)
            )


    return elements



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Projection to orbit
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def orbit_projection(mu):


    best=None

    best_dist=1e99


    for x in orbit_elements(mu):


        d=np.linalg.norm(
            x-mu_star
        )


        if d<best_dist:

            best_dist=d
            best=x



    return best



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Contractive flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    target=orbit_projection(mu)


    return (
        target
        +
        rho*(mu-target)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil equivariance
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5



print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Weyl orbit convergence")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(10):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            mu-mu_star
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Satake
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)



print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(10):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part74 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Weyl orbit projection converges

and

Weil involution is preserved

and

Satake spectrum is recovered

then:

Recovery-Satake Weyl orbit fixed point established.

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part75
#
# Weil Equivariant Pair Projection
#
# Canonical Satake orbit recovery
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25

])



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Canonical pair projection
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def PairProjection(mu):


    result=np.zeros(6)


    for k in range(3):

        i=2*k
        j=2*k+1


        m=(mu[i]-mu[j])/2


        # preserve orientation

        result[i]=m
        result[j]=-m


    return result



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    target=PairProjection(mu)


    return (
        target
        +
        rho*(mu-target)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Weil equivariance
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Pair fixed point flow")
print("="*60)


mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(10):

    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star)
    )

    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Satake
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(10):

        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part76
#
# Center-Difference Weil Recovery
#
# Canonical pair coordinate flow
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25

])



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Pair coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def pair_coordinates(mu):


    c=[]
    d=[]


    for k in range(3):

        i=2*k
        j=2*k+1


        c.append(
            (mu[i]+mu[j])/2
        )


        d.append(
            (mu[i]-mu[j])/2
        )


    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def reconstruct(c,d):


    mu=np.zeros(6)


    for k in range(3):

        mu[2*k]=c[k]+d[k]

        mu[2*k+1]=c[k]-d[k]


    return mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


target_c,target_d=pair_coordinates(mu_star)



def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    # Weil removes center

    c_new=(
        rho*c
    )


    # Satake difference recovery

    d_new=(
        target_d
        +
        rho*(d-target_d)
    )


    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Equivariance
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5



print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Center-Difference convergence")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(12):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    print(
        n,
        "mu error",
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star),
        "center",
        np.linalg.norm(c),
        "diff",
        np.linalg.norm(d-target_d)
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Satake
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



print("\n")

Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)

center variables recover Weil symmetry

and

difference variables recover Satake phase

Canonical Weil-Satake coordinate flow established.
