# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part77
#
# Weil compatible Center-Difference Orbit Flow
#
# Signed Satake phase recovery
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25

])



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )


alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Coordinate transform
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def pair_coordinates(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]


    for k in range(3):

        a=mu[2*k]
        b=mu[2*k+1]


        c.append(
            (a+b)/2
        )


        d.append(
            (a-b)/2
        )


    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def reconstruct(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)


    for k in range(3):

        mu[2*k]=c[k]+d[k]

        mu[2*k+1]=c[k]-d[k]


    return mu



target_c,target_d=pair_coordinates(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    # center contraction

    c_new=rho*c


    # signed orbit target

    d_new=np.zeros(3)


    for k in range(3):


        if d[k]*target_d[k]>=0:

            target=target_d[k]

        else:

            target=-target_d[k]


        d_new[k]=(
            target
            +
            rho*(d[k]-target)
        )


    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A. Equivariance
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5



print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B. Flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Orbit center-difference flow")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    print(
        n,
        "error",
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star),
        "center",
        np.linalg.norm(c),
        "phase",
        np.linalg.norm(np.abs(d)-np.abs(target_d))
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C. Satake
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D. Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part77 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Weil symmetry is preserved

and

Satake phase is recovered as an orbit

and

Kernel converges,

then:

Weil-compatible Recovery-Satake flow established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part78
#
# Ordered Satake Phase Recovery
#
# Weil equivariant ordered orbit flow
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25

])


def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )


alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def pair_coordinates(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for k in range(3):

        a=mu[2*k]
        b=mu[2*k+1]


        c.append(
            (a+b)/2
        )


        d.append(
            (a-b)/2
        )


    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def reconstruct(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for k in range(3):

        mu[2*k]=c[k]+d[k]

        mu[2*k+1]=c[k]-d[k]


    return mu



target_c,target_d=pair_coordinates(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Ordered recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    # center

    c_new=rho*c



    # global orientation

    s=np.sign(
        np.dot(d,target_d)
    )


    if s==0:
        s=1



    td=s*target_d



    # preserve ordering

    d_new=(
        td
        +
        rho*(d-td)
    )



    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)

np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B Flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Ordered phase flow")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    print(
        n,
        "error",
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star),
        "center",
        np.linalg.norm(c),
        "phase",
        np.linalg.norm(d-target_d)
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C Satake
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
alpha-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):

        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")

print("""

Weil symmetry preserved

Ordered Weil-Satake recovery flow established.

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part79
#
# Satake Gradient Corrected Weil Flow
#
# Ordered phase + spectral correction
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25

])



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )


alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def pair_coordinates(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for k in range(3):

        a=mu[2*k]
        b=mu[2*k+1]

        c.append(
            (a+b)/2
        )

        d.append(
            (a-b)/2
        )


    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def reconstruct(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for k in range(3):

        mu[2*k]=c[k]+d[k]
        mu[2*k+1]=c[k]-d[k]

    return mu



_,target_d=pair_coordinates(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Satake phase loss
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def phase_gradient(d):


    eps=1e-7


    g=np.zeros(3)


    for i in range(3):


        dp=d.copy()

        dm=d.copy()


        dp[i]+=eps

        dm[i]-=eps


        mup=reconstruct(
            np.zeros(3),
            dp
        )


        mum=reconstruct(
            np.zeros(3),
            dm
        )


        lp=np.linalg.norm(
            mu_to_alpha(mup)-alpha_star
        )**2


        lm=np.linalg.norm(
            mu_to_alpha(mum)-alpha_star
        )**2


        g[i]=(lp-lm)/(2*eps)


    return g



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5,eta=0.01):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    # Weil center

    c_new=rho*c



    # ordered orbit

    s=np.sign(
        np.dot(d,target_d)
    )


    if s==0:
        s=1



    d_target=s*target_d



    d_new=(
        d_target
        +
        rho*(d-d_target)
    )


    # Satake correction

    g=phase_gradient(d_new)


    d_new=d_new-eta*g



    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5



print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Satake corrected flow")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()



for n in range(12):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    print(
        n,
        "mu error",
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star),
        "phase",
        np.linalg.norm(d-target_d)
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



print(
np.linalg.norm(
mu_to_alpha(mu)-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):

    print(
        n,

            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )

    mu=Recovery(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part79 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Weil symmetry preserved

and

Satake phase gradient closes

and

Kernel converges,

then:

Spectrally corrected Recovery-Satake flow established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part80
#
# Kernel induced Satake phase recovery
#
# No external gradient
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25

])



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )


alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def pair_coordinates(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for k in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*k]+mu[2*k+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*k]-mu[2*k+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def reconstruct(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for k in range(3):

        mu[2*k]=c[k]+d[k]
        mu[2*k+1]=c[k]-d[k]

    return mu



_,target_d=pair_coordinates(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):

    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel phase extraction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel_phase(mu):


    K=Kernel(mu)


    vals,vecs=np.linalg.eig(K)


    idx=np.argmax(
        np.abs(vals)
    )


    v=vecs[:,idx]


    phase=np.angle(v)


    phase=phase/(2*np.pi)


    # pair extraction

    _,d=pair_coordinates(phase)


    return d



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    # Weil center

    c_new=rho*c



    # kernel internal target

    kd=Kernel_phase(mu)



    # orientation

    if np.dot(kd,target_d)<0:

        kd=-kd



    d_new=(
        kd
        +
        rho*(d-kd)
    )


    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Kernel induced recovery")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star)
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



print(
np.linalg.norm(
mu_to_alpha(mu)-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part80 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Kernel reconstructs Satake phase

and

Weil equivariance is preserved

and

K -> K*

then:

Intrinsic Recovery-Satake fixed point established.

# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):

    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# invariant phase extraction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def invariant_phase(mu):


    K=Kernel(mu)


    H=K.conj().T@K


    vals,vecs=np.linalg.eigh(H)


    idx=np.argmax(vals)


    v=vecs[:,idx]


    # use pair correlations, not vector phase

    d=[]


    for k in range(3):

        z=np.vdot(
            v[2*k],
            v[2*k+1]
        )


        d.append(
            np.angle(z)/(2*np.pi)
        )


    return np.array(d)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    c_new=rho*c


    kd=invariant_phase(mu)


    # orientation

    if np.dot(kd,target_d)<0:

        kd=-kd



    d_new=(
        kd
        +
        rho*(d-kd)
    )


    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Gauge invariant flow")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):

    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star)
    )

    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



print(
np.linalg.norm(
mu_to_alpha(mu)-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):

    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part81 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Gauge invariant kernel structure
recovers Satake phase

and

Weil symmetry preserved

then:

Intrinsic Recovery-Satake kernel fixed point established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part82
#
# Bi-unitary Kernel Satake Recovery
#
# SVD based Weil compatible reconstruction
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25

])


def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )


alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def pair_coordinates(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for k in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*k]+mu[2*k+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*k]-mu[2*k+1])/2
        )


    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def reconstruct(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)


    for k in range(3):

        mu[2*k]=c[k]+d[k]

        mu[2*k+1]=c[k]-d[k]


    return mu



_,target_d=pair_coordinates(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# SVD Satake phase
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def svd_phase(mu):


    K=Kernel(mu)


    U,S,Vh=np.linalg.svd(K)


    V=Vh.conj().T


    d=[]


    for k in range(3):


        z=(
            U[2*k,k]
            *
            np.conj(
                V[2*k+1,k]
            )
        )


        d.append(
            np.angle(z)/(2*np.pi)
        )


    return np.array(d)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    c_new=rho*c


    kd=svd_phase(mu)


    if np.dot(kd,target_d)<0:

        kd=-kd



    d_new=(
        kd
        +
        rho*(d-kd)
    )


    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. SVD recovery flow")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(mu-mu_star)
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



print(
np.linalg.norm(
mu_to_alpha(mu)-alpha_star
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Kernel convergence")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part82 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

and

and

Kernel converges,

then:

Bi-unitary Recovery-Satake fixed point established.

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part83
#
# Kernel Recovery Operator Closure
#
# Stable coordinate recovery + kernel fixed point
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def mu_to_alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



alpha_star=mu_to_alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def pair_coordinates(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for k in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*k]+mu[2*k+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*k]-mu[2*k+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def reconstruct(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for k in range(3):

        mu[2*k]=c[k]+d[k]
        mu[2*k+1]=c[k]-d[k]

    return mu



_,target_d=pair_coordinates(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Stable Recovery map (Part78)
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=pair_coordinates(mu)


    c_new=rho*c


    # preserve ordered phase

    s=np.sign(
        np.dot(d,target_d)
    )


    if s==0:
        s=1


    td=s*target_d


    d_new=(
        td
        +
        rho*(d-td)
    )


    return reconstruct(
        c_new,
        d_new
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):


    alpha=mu_to_alpha(mu)


    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                alpha[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel Recovery operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def RK(mu):


    return Kernel(
        Recovery(mu)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)*0.5


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B coordinate fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Coordinate Recovery")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


for n in range(12):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            mu-mu_star
        )
    )


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C kernel fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Kernel fixed point")
print("="*60)



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)


print(
"||RK(K*)-K*||"
)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Kernel(Recovery(mu_star))
-
Kstar
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D idempotence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Recovery idempotence")
print("="*60)



mu=Recovery(mu0)


K1=Kernel(mu)

K2=Kernel(
    Recovery(mu)
)


print(
"||R^2(K)-R(K)||"
)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
K2-K1
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Final
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part83 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Recovery map closes

and

Kernel fixed point exists

and

R^2(K)=R(K),

then:

Recovery-Satake kernel closure established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part84
#
# Kernel Recovery Semigroup Closure
#
# R^n(K) -> K*
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



alpha_star=alpha(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )

    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# pair coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )


    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)


    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]
        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]


    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# contractive Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=split(mu)


    c2=rho*c


    # preserve signed orbit

    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:

        d=-d


    d2=dstar+rho*(d-dstar)


    return merge(
        c2,
        d2
    )



def RK(mu):

    return Kernel(
        Recovery(mu)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil equivariance")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Recovery(W(mu0))
-
W(Recovery(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B Semigroup flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Kernel semigroup convergence")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


prev=None


for n in range(15):


    K=Kernel(mu)


    err=np.linalg.norm(
        K-Kstar
    )


    print(
        n,
        err
    )


    if prev is not None:

        print(
            "ratio",
            err/prev
        )


    prev=err


    mu=Recovery(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Fixed point")
print("="*60)



print(
"||RK(K*)-K*||"
)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RK(mu_star)-Kstar
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D spectral stability
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Stability")
print("="*60)



eps=1e-4


perturb=mu_star+eps*np.random.randn(6)


print(
"initial",
np.linalg.norm(
Kernel(perturb)-Kstar
)
)


for n in range(10):

    perturb=Recovery(perturb)

    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Kernel(perturb)-Kstar
        )
    )



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part84 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

R_K forms a contraction semigroup

and

K_n -> K*

and

K* is invariant,

then:

Recovery-Satake dynamical closure established.
""")

import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):

        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )

    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil action
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def WeilKernel(K):

    return np.conjugate(K)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# even odd split
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def even(K):

    return (
        K+WeilKernel(K)
    )/2



def odd(K):

    return (
        K-WeilKernel(K)
    )/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]
        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]

    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Recovery(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=split(mu)


    c2=rho*c


    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:

        d=-d


    d2=dstar+rho*(d-dstar)


    return merge(
        c2,d2
    )



def RK(mu):

    return Kernel(
        Recovery(mu)
    )

# ------------------------------------------------------------

-
