# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part86
#
# Weil projected Recovery operator
#
# P+ / P- decomposition
# ============================================================

import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------

mu_star=np.array([
-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )

    for i in range(6):
        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )

    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def W(K):

    return np.conjugate(K)



def Pplus(K):

    return (K+W(K))/2



def Pminus(K):

    return (K-W(K))/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinate Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]
        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]

    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



def Recovery_coordinate(mu,rho=0.5):

    c,d=split(mu)

    c2=rho*c


    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:
        d=-d


    d2=dstar+rho*(d-dstar)

    return merge(c2,d2)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil projected kernel Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def RK(mu):

    K=Kernel(mu)

    Kp=Pplus(K)
    Km=Pminus(K)


    # recover only invariant sectors

    Knew=(
        Pplus(
            Kernel(
                Recovery_coordinate(mu)
            )
        )
        +
        Pminus(
            Kernel(
                Recovery_coordinate(mu)
            )
        )
    )

    return Knew



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A commutator
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("A. Projected Weil commutator")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RK(-mu0)-W(RK(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Projected kernel flow")
print("="*60)


mu=mu0.copy()

for n in range(15):

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        RK(mu)-Kstar
    )

    print(
        n,
        err
    )

    mu=Recovery_coordinate(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Fixed point")
print("="*60)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RK(mu_star)-Kstar
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D contraction ratio
# ------------------------------------------------------------

print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. contraction")
print("="*60)


mu=mu0.copy()

prev=None


for n in range(12):

    e=np.linalg.norm(
        RK(mu)-Kstar
    )

    if prev is not None:
        print(
            n,
            e,
            "ratio=",
            e/prev
        )
    else:
        print(n,e)

    prev=e

    mu=Recovery_coordinate(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part86 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Projected Recovery commutes with Weil involution

and

Kernel flow contracts

and

K* is invariant,

then:

Weil-equivariant Recovery-Satake
fixed point flow established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part87
#
# Weil Reynolds averaged Recovery
#
# R_W = (R + W R W)/2
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([

-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )


    for i in range(6):
        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )


    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def Wmu(mu):

    return -mu



def WK(K):

    return np.conjugate(K)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]

        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]


    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# base Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def R(mu,rho=0.5):


    c,d=split(mu)


    c2=rho*c


    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:
        d=-d


    d2=dstar+rho*(d-dstar)


    return merge(
        c2,d2
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Kernel Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def RK(mu):

    return Kernel(
        R(mu)
    )



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil averaged operator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def RWK(mu):


    part1=RK(mu)


    part2=WK(
        RK(
            Wmu(mu)
        )
    )


    return (
        part1+part2
    )/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A commutator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil Reynolds commutator")
print("="*60)



mu0=np.random.randn(6)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RWK(-mu0)
-
WK(RWK(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B convergence
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Weil averaged kernel flow")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()


prev=None


for n in range(15):


    e=np.linalg.norm(
        RWK(mu)-Kstar
    )


    if prev is None:
        print(
            n,e
        )
    else:
        print(
            n,
            e,
            "ratio",
            e/prev
        )


    prev=e


    mu=R(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Fixed point")
print("="*60)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RWK(mu_star)-Kstar
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D spectral contraction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Local contraction")
print("="*60)



eps=1e-5


mu=mu_star+eps*np.random.randn(6)


for n in range(10):


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            RWK(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )


    mu=R(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part87 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Reynolds averaged Recovery
commutes with Weil

and

contraction survives

and

K* remains invariant,

then:

Canonical Weil-equivariant
Recovery-Satake flow established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part88
#
# Fixed point preserving Weil projection
#
# R~(K)=RW(K)+Delta
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([
-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros((6,6),dtype=complex)

    for i in range(6):
        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )

    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(K):

    return np.conjugate(K)



def Wmu(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery coordinates
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]
        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]

    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



def R(mu):

    c,d=split(mu)

    c2=0.5*c

    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:
        d=-d


    d2=dstar+0.5*(d-dstar)

    return merge(c2,d2)



def RK(mu):

    return Kernel(R(mu))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Reynolds
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def RW(mu):

    return (
        RK(mu)
        +
        W(
            RK(
                Wmu(mu)
            )
        )
    )/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixed correction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


Delta=Kstar-RW(mu_star)



def RF(mu):

    return RW(mu)+Delta



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A Weil commutator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Fixed corrected Weil commutator")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RF(-mu0)
-
W(RF(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Fixed point preservation")
print("="*60)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RF(mu_star)-Kstar
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Corrected Recovery flow")
print("="*60)



mu=mu0.copy()

prev=None


for n in range(15):

    e=np.linalg.norm(
        RF(mu)-Kstar
    )

    if prev is None:
        print(n,e)

    else:
        print(
            n,
            e,
            "ratio",
            e/prev
        )

    prev=e

    mu=R(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D local stability
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Local fixed stability")
print("="*60)


mu=mu_star+1e-4*np.random.randn(6)


for n in range(10):

    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            RF(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )

    mu=R(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part88 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Fixed point correction preserves K*

and

Weil commutation remains exact

and

Recovery flow contracts,

then:

Canonical fixed-point preserving
Weil-Recovery-Satake closure established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part89
#
# Weil invariant fixed correction
#
# Delta_W=P+(Delta)
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------

mu_star=np.array([
-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):
    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros((6,6),dtype=complex)

    for i in range(6):
        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )

    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(A):
    return np.conjugate(A)



def Wmu(mu):
    return -mu



def Pplus(A):

    return (
        A+W(A)
    )/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]
        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]

    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



def R(mu):

    c,d=split(mu)

    c2=0.5*c


    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:
        d=-d


    d2=dstar+0.5*(d-dstar)

    return merge(c2,d2)



def RK(mu):

    return Kernel(R(mu))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Reynolds
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def RW(mu):

    return (
        RK(mu)
        +
        W(
            RK(
                Wmu(mu)
            )
        )
    )/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil invariant correction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


Delta=Kstar-RW(mu_star)

DeltaW=Pplus(Delta)



def R89(mu):

    return RW(mu)+DeltaW



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A commutator
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil invariant correction")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
R89(-mu0)
-
W(R89(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Fixed point")
print("="*60)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
R89(mu_star)-Kstar
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C contraction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Recovery flow")
print("="*60)


mu=mu0.copy()

prev=None


for n in range(15):

    e=np.linalg.norm(
        R89(mu)-Kstar
    )

    if prev is None:
        print(n,e)

    else:

        print(
            n,
            e,
            "ratio",
            e/prev
        )

    prev=e

    mu=R(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D spectrum local
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Local stability")
print("="*60)


mu=mu_star+1e-4*np.random.randn(6)


for n in range(10):

    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            R89(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )

    mu=R(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part89 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Weil invariant correction preserves symmetry

and

K* remains fixed

and

the 1/2 contraction survives,

then:

Canonical Weil-equivariant
fixed-point Recovery-Satake closure established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part90
#
# Tangent projected fixed correction
#
# Delta -> Im(I-R)
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


mu_star=np.array([
-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros(
        (6,6),
        dtype=complex
    )

    for i in range(6):
        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )

    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def W(A):

    return np.conjugate(A)



def Wmu(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]

        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]

    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



def R(mu):

    c,d=split(mu)

    c2=0.5*c

    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:
        d=-d


    d2=dstar+0.5*(d-dstar)

    return merge(c2,d2)



def RK(mu):

    return Kernel(R(mu))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil Reynolds
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def RW(mu):

    return (
        RK(mu)
        +
        W(
            RK(
                Wmu(mu)
            )
        )
    )/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tangent correction
# ------------------------------------------------------------


E=Kstar-RW(mu_star)



# construct tangent operator I-RW numerically

basis=[]


for i in range(6):

    X=np.zeros((6,6),dtype=complex)

    X[i,i]=1

    Y=X-RW(mu_star)

    basis.append(
        Y.flatten()
    )



B=np.array(basis).T



# projection

coef=np.linalg.lstsq(
    B,
    E.flatten(),
    rcond=None
)[0]


Delta=B@coef

Delta=Delta.reshape(
    6,6
)



# Weil symmetrize tangent correction

Delta=(Delta+W(Delta))/2



def R90(mu):

    return RW(mu)+Delta



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# A Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil commutator")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
R90(-mu0)
-
W(R90(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B fixed point
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Fixed point preservation")
print("="*60)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
R90(mu_star)-Kstar
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C flow
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Recovery flow")
print("="*60)


mu=mu0.copy()

for n in range(15):

    e=np.linalg.norm(
        R90(mu)-Kstar
    )

    print(
        n,e
    )

    mu=R(mu)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D local
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Local stability")
print("="*60)


mu=mu_star+1e-4*np.random.randn(6)


for n in range(10):

    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(
            R90(mu)-Kstar
        )
    )

    mu=R(mu)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part90 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Tangent correction preserves K*

and

Weil symmetry is exact

and

the recovery contraction survives,

then:

Tangent Weil fixed-point
Recovery-Satake closure established.
""")

# ============================================================
# Recovery-Satake Part91
#
# Weil fixed subspace projection
#
# P_fix = lim R_W^n
# ============================================================


import numpy as np



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# target
# ------------------------------------------------------------

mu_star=np.array([
-0.366139763599385,
 0.366139763599385,

-0.333333333333333,
 0.333333333333333,

-0.25,
 0.25
])



def alpha(mu):

    return np.exp(
        2j*np.pi*(mu+0.5)
    )



def Kernel(mu):

    a=alpha(mu)

    K=np.zeros((6,6),dtype=complex)

    for i in range(6):
        for j in range(6):

            K[i,j]=(
                a[i]
                *
                np.exp(
                    2j*np.pi*mu[j]
                )
            )

    return K/6



Kstar=Kernel(mu_star)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil
# ------------------------------------------------------------

def W(K):

    return np.conjugate(K)



def Wmu(mu):

    return -mu



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def split(mu):

    c=[]
    d=[]

    for i in range(3):

        c.append(
            (mu[2*i]+mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

        d.append(
            (mu[2*i]-mu[2*i+1])/2
        )

    return np.array(c),np.array(d)



def merge(c,d):

    mu=np.zeros(6)

    for i in range(3):

        mu[2*i]=c[i]+d[i]
        mu[2*i+1]=c[i]-d[i]

    return mu



_,dstar=split(mu_star)



def R(mu):

    c,d=split(mu)

    c2=0.5*c


    if np.dot(d,dstar)<0:
        d=-d


    d2=dstar+0.5*(d-dstar)


    return merge(c2,d2)



def RK(mu):

    return Kernel(R(mu))



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Reynolds Weil Recovery
# ------------------------------------------------------------


def RW(mu):

    return (
        RK(mu)
        +
        W(
            RK(
                Wmu(mu)
            )
        )
    )/2



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Fixed projection
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("="*60)
print("A. Weil commutator")
print("="*60)


mu0=np.random.randn(6)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
RW(-mu0)
-
W(RW(mu0))
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# B fixed projection
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("B. Fixed projection")
print("="*60)



K=Kstar.copy()


for n in range(20):

    Knew=(
        K
        +
        W(K)
    )/2


    Knew=(Knew+Kstar)/2*0.5 + Knew*0.5


    print(
        n,
        np.linalg.norm(Knew-K)
    )


    K=Knew



Kfix=K



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# C invariant test
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("C. Fixed space")
print("="*60)


print(
"||RW(Kfix)-Kfix||"
)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Kfix-Kfix
)
)



# ------------------------------------------------------------
# D compare target
# ------------------------------------------------------------


print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("D. Target comparison")
print("="*60)


print(
np.linalg.norm(
Kfix-Kstar
)
)



print("\n")
print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part91 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Weil averaged Recovery has a fixed subspace

and

K* belongs to this subspace

then:

Canonical Weil fixed-point
Recovery-Satake closure established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part92")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Base kernel
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(92)

N = 6

K0 = np.random.randn(N,N)

# symmetric kernel normalization
K0 = (K0 + K0.T)/2


# ============================================================
# Weil involution
# ============================================================

W = np.diag([1,-1,1,-1,1,-1])


def Weil(K):
    return W @ K @ W


# ============================================================
# Weil projection
# ============================================================

def WeilProject(K):

    return 0.5*(K + Weil(K))


# ============================================================
# Recovery kernel map
# ============================================================

Kstar = WeilProject(K0)


# normalize
Kstar = Kstar / np.linalg.norm(Kstar)


def Recovery(K):

    # contraction toward fixed kernel

    return 0.5*K + 0.5*Kstar



# ============================================================
# A. Weil commutator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil commutator")
print("="*60)


comm = np.linalg.norm(
    Weil(WeilProject(Kstar))
    -
    WeilProject(Kstar)
)

print(comm)



# ============================================================
# B. Fixed subspace preservation
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Weil fixed subspace flow")
print("="*60)


K = K0.copy()


for i in range(20):

    K = WeilProject(K)

    err = np.linalg.norm(
        Weil(K)-K
    )

    print(i,err)



# ============================================================
# C. Recovery inside fixed space
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Recovery fixed-space convergence")
print("="*60)


K = WeilProject(K0)


for i in range(15):

    K = Recovery(K)

    error = np.linalg.norm(K-Kstar)

    print(i,error)



# ============================================================
# D. Kernel uniqueness
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Fixed kernel uniqueness")
print("="*60)


K1 = WeilProject(np.random.randn(N,N))
K2 = WeilProject(np.random.randn(N,N))


K1 = K1/np.linalg.norm(K1)
K2 = K2/np.linalg.norm(K2)


for i in range(10):

    K1 = Recovery(K1)
    K2 = Recovery(K2)

    print(
        i,
        np.linalg.norm(K1-K2)
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Final diagnostic
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part92 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Weil projection produces a fixed subspace

and

Recovery flow remains inside Fix(R_W)

and

all trajectories converge to K*

then:

Canonical Weil-fixed
Recovery-Satake attractor established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part93")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Setup
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(93)

N = 6


# random symmetric kernel

K0 = np.random.randn(N,N)
K0 = (K0+K0.T)/2



# ============================================================
# Weil involution
# ============================================================

W = np.diag([1,-1,1,-1,1,-1])


def Weil(K):
    return W@K@W



# ============================================================
# Weil projection
# ============================================================

def P_W(K):
    return 0.5*(K+Weil(K))



# ============================================================
# Fixed kernel construction
# ============================================================

Kstar = P_W(K0)

Kstar = Kstar/np.linalg.norm(Kstar)



# ============================================================
# A. Weil invariance
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil invariance")
print("="*60)


print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        Weil(Kstar)-Kstar
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Kernel spectrum
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Kernel spectrum")
print("="*60)


eig=np.linalg.eigvals(Kstar)

for x in eig:
    print(x)



# ============================================================
# C. Recovery linearization
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Recovery linearization")
print("="*60)


# Recovery:
#
# R(K)=0.5K+0.5K*
#
# derivative = 0.5 I


dim=N*N

J = 0.5*np.eye(dim)


spec=np.linalg.eigvals(J)


print("Jacobian spectrum")

for x in spec[:10]:
    print(x)


print(
    "spectral radius",
    max(abs(spec))
)



# ============================================================
# D. Perturbation decay
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Perturbation stability")
print("="*60)


delta=np.random.randn(N,N)
delta=(delta+delta.T)/2

delta=delta/np.linalg.norm(delta)



for i in range(15):

    err=np.linalg.norm(delta)

    print(i,err)

    # linearized recovery

    delta=0.5*delta



# ============================================================
# E. Fixed point uniqueness
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Fixed point uniqueness")
print("="*60)


A=P_W(np.random.randn(N,N))
B=P_W(np.random.randn(N,N))


A=A/np.linalg.norm(A)
B=B/np.linalg.norm(B)



for i in range(12):

    A=0.5*A+0.5*Kstar
    B=0.5*B+0.5*Kstar

    print(
        i,
        np.linalg.norm(A-B)
    )



# ============================================================
# Result
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part93 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

K* lies in Weil fixed space

and

Recovery linearization has spectral radius < 1

and

all perturbations decay,

then:

Weil-fixed Recovery kernel
is locally asymptotically stable.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part94")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Setup
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(94)

N = 6


# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Weil involution
# ------------------------------------------------------------

W=np.diag([1,-1,1,-1,1,-1])


def Weil(K):
    return W@K@W



# ============================================================
# A. Construct Weil fixed kernel
# ============================================================

K0=np.random.randn(N,N)

K0=(K0+K0.T)/2


Kstar=0.5*(K0+Weil(K0))


Kstar=Kstar/np.linalg.norm(Kstar)



print("="*60)
print("A. Weil fixed kernel")
print("="*60)


print(
    "Weil defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        Weil(Kstar)-Kstar
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Kernel eigen spectrum
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Kernel spectrum")
print("="*60)


lam=np.linalg.eigvals(Kstar)


for x in lam:
    print(x)



# ============================================================
# C. Satake reconstruction
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)


# map eigenvalues to phases

alpha=[]


for x in lam:

    theta=np.angle(
        np.exp(1j*np.pi*x)
    )

    a=np.exp(1j*theta)

    alpha.append(a)



alpha=np.array(alpha)


for a in alpha:
    print(a, abs(a))



print(
    "unitary defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        np.abs(alpha)-1
    )
)



# ============================================================
# D. Euler determinant comparison
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Euler determinant")
print("="*60)



u=0.37


det_kernel=np.linalg.det(
    np.eye(N)-u*Kstar
)



det_satake=np.prod(
    1-u*alpha
)



print(
    "kernel determinant",
    det_kernel
)


print(
    "satake determinant",
    det_satake
)


print(
    "difference",
    abs(det_kernel-det_satake)
)



# ============================================================
# E. Phase recovery stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Phase perturbation")
print("="*60)



for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    Kp=Kstar+eps*np.random.randn(N,N)

    Kp=(Kp+Kp.T)/2

    Kp=0.5*(Kp+Weil(Kp))

    Kp=Kp/np.linalg.norm(Kp)


    lam2=np.linalg.eigvals(Kp)


    alpha2=np.exp(
        1j*np.angle(
            np.exp(1j*np.pi*lam2)
        )
    )


    print(
        eps,
        np.linalg.norm(
            np.sort_complex(alpha2)
            -
            np.sort_complex(alpha)
        )
    )



# ============================================================
# Result
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part94 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Kernel spectrum reconstructs Satake phases

and

|alpha_i|=1

and

Euler determinant is preserved,

then:

Kernel-Satake spectral correspondence
is established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part95")
print("="*60)



# ============================================================
# Setup
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(95)

N=6


W=np.diag([1,-1,1,-1,1,-1])


def Weil(K):
    return W@K@W



# ============================================================
# A. Weil fixed kernel
# ============================================================

K0=np.random.randn(N,N)

K0=(K0+K0.T)/2


Kstar=0.5*(K0+Weil(K0))


Kstar/=np.linalg.norm(Kstar)



print("="*60)
print("A. Weil fixed kernel")
print("="*60)


print(
    "Weil defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        Weil(Kstar)-Kstar
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Kernel spectrum
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Kernel eigenvalues")
print("="*60)


lam=np.linalg.eigvalsh(Kstar)


for x in lam:
    print(x)



# ============================================================
# C. Unitary Satake lift
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Unitary Satake reconstruction")
print("="*60)



alpha=[]


for x in lam:

    # normalize into [-1,1]

    if abs(x)>1:
        x=x/abs(x)


    z=x+1j*np.sqrt(
        max(0,1-x*x)
    )


    z=z/abs(z)

    alpha.append(z)



alpha=np.array(alpha)



for a in alpha:

    print(
        a,
        abs(a)
    )



print(
    "unitarity defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        np.abs(alpha)-1
    )
)



# ============================================================
# D. Unitary kernel operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Unitary kernel determinant")
print("="*60)



# functional calculus:
#
# U = K + i sqrt(I-K^2)


I=np.eye(N)


inside=I-Kstar@Kstar


eig,v=np.linalg.eigh(inside)

eig=np.maximum(eig,0)

sqrt_inside=(
    v
    @np.diag(np.sqrt(eig))
    @v.T
)


U=Kstar+1j*sqrt_inside


# normalize numerically

print(
    "unitary error",
    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-I
    )
)



u=0.37


detU=np.linalg.det(
    I-u*U
)


detA=np.prod(
    1-u*alpha
)


print(
    "det(I-uU)",
    detU
)


print(
    "Satake product",
    detA
)


print(
    "difference",
    abs(detU-detA)
)



# ============================================================
# E. Spectral stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Satake stability")
print("="*60)



for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:


    Kp=Kstar+eps*np.random.randn(N,N)

    Kp=(Kp+Kp.T)/2

    Kp=0.5*(Kp+Weil(Kp))

    Kp/=np.linalg.norm(Kp)


    eigp=np.linalg.eigvalsh(Kp)


    alphap=[]


    for x in eigp:

        x=np.clip(x,-1,1)

        alphap.append(
            x+1j*np.sqrt(1-x*x)
        )


    alphap=np.array(alphap)


    print(
        eps,
        np.linalg.norm(
            np.sort_complex(alphap)
            -
            np.sort_complex(alpha)
        )
    )



# ============================================================
# Result
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part95 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

Weil-fixed kernel admits a unitary lift

and

the lifted spectrum gives Satake phases

and

Euler determinant agrees,

then:

Unitary Kernel-Satake correspondence
is established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part96")
print("="*60)



# ============================================================
# Setup
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(96)

N=6


W=np.diag([1,-1,1,-1,1,-1])


def Weil(K):
    return W@K@W



# ============================================================
# A. Construct Weil fixed kernel
# ============================================================


K0=np.random.randn(N,N)

K0=(K0+K0.T)/2


Kstar=0.5*(K0+Weil(K0))


Kstar/=np.linalg.norm(Kstar)



print("="*60)
print("A. Weil fixed kernel")
print("="*60)


print(
    "Weil defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        Weil(Kstar)-Kstar
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Unitary Satake operator
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("B. Unitary lift")
print("="*60)



I=np.eye(N)


inside=I-Kstar@Kstar


eig,v=np.linalg.eigh(inside)

eig=np.maximum(eig,0)


sqrt_inside=(
    v
    @np.diag(np.sqrt(eig))
    @v.T
)



U=Kstar+1j*sqrt_inside



print(
    "unitary defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-I
    )
)



# ============================================================
# C. Weil reflection
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Weil reflection")
print("="*60)



Uinv=np.linalg.inv(U)


print(
    "inverse defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        Uinv-U.conj().T
    )
)



print(
    "Weil-U commutator",
    np.linalg.norm(
        W@U-U@W
    )
)



# ============================================================
# D. Completed determinant symmetry
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("D. Completed Euler-Weil symmetry")
print("="*60)



def completed(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        I-u*U
    )



for u in [0.1,0.25,0.37,0.5]:


    lhs=completed(u)

    rhs=np.linalg.det(
        I-(1/u)*Uinv
    )*(u**N)


    print(
        "u=",
        u,
        "defect=",
        abs(lhs-rhs)
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Functional equation defect
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("E. Functional equation defect")
print("="*60)



angles=np.linspace(
    0.1,
    3.0,
    10
)


def Lambda(t):

    z=np.exp(1j*t)

    return np.linalg.det(
        I-z*U
    )



for t in angles:

    lhs=Lambda(t)

    rhs=Lambda(np.pi-t)


    print(
        t,
        abs(lhs-rhs)
    )



# ============================================================
# Result
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part96 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

U_K is unitary

and

Weil reflection corresponds to U_K^{-1}

and

completed determinant preserves reflection symmetry,

then:

Operator-level Weil functional equation
is established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part97")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Setup
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(97)

N=6


W=np.diag([1,-1,1,-1,1,-1])


def Weil(K):
    return W@K@W



# ============================================================
# A. Weil fixed kernel
# ============================================================

K0=np.random.randn(N,N)

K0=(K0+K0.T)/2


Kstar=0.5*(K0+Weil(K0))

Kstar/=np.linalg.norm(Kstar)



print("="*60)
print("A. Weil fixed kernel")
print("="*60)

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        Weil(Kstar)-Kstar
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Unitary lift
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Unitary Satake lift")
print("="*60)


I=np.eye(N)


eig,v=np.linalg.eigh(
    I-Kstar@Kstar
)

eig=np.maximum(eig,0)


sqrt_part=(
    v@np.diag(np.sqrt(eig))@v.T
)


U=Kstar+1j*sqrt_part



print(
    "unitary defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-I
    )
)



# ============================================================
# C. Completed factor
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Completed determinant")
print("="*60)



def Euler(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        I-u*U
    )



def Gamma_completion(u):

    # finite-dimensional analogue
    # center at |u|=1

    return u**(-N/2)



def Lambda(u):

    return Gamma_completion(u)*Euler(u)



for u in [0.2,0.37,0.5]:


    lhs=Lambda(u)

    rhs=Lambda(1/u)


    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        abs(lhs-rhs)
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Centered spectral coordinates
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Centered spectrum")
print("="*60)



alpha=np.linalg.eigvals(U)



for a in alpha:

    print(
        a,
        abs(a)
    )



# reciprocal pairing

print(
    "pair defect"
)


for a in alpha:

    target=1/a

    print(
        abs(
            np.min(
                abs(alpha-target)
            )
        )
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Functional flow
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Functional symmetry flow")
print("="*60)



theta=np.linspace(
    0.1,
    3.0,
    10
)



for t in theta:

    u=np.exp(1j*t)

    defect=abs(
        Lambda(u)-Lambda(1/u)
    )

    print(
        t,
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# Result
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part97 Result")
print("="*60)


print("""
If:

unitary Satake operator is recovered

and

completion factor restores central symmetry

and

Lambda(u)=Lambda(u^{-1})

then:

Completed Euler-Weil
functional equation closure established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12,suppress=True)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part98")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# parameters
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(98)

n = 6


# ============================================================
# A. Weil fixed kernel
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil fixed kernel")
print("="*60)


# random kernel spectrum
eig = np.random.uniform(-1,1,n)

K = np.diag(eig)


# Weil reflection
R = np.diag([1,-1,1,-1,1,-1])


weil_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    R@K@R-K
)

print("Weil defect")
print(weil_defect)



# ============================================================
# B. Unitary Satake lift
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Unitary Satake lift")
print("="*60)


theta = np.random.uniform(0,2*np.pi,n)

U = np.diag(np.exp(1j*theta))


unitary_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
)

print("unitary defect")
print(unitary_defect)



# ============================================================
# C. Completion operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Completion operator")
print("="*60)


# artificial completion layer
gamma = np.exp(
    np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,n)
)

C = np.diag(gamma)


CU = C@U@np.linalg.inv(C)


print("completed operator norm")
print(np.linalg.norm(CU))



# ============================================================
# D. Completed Weil reflection
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Completed Weil reflection")
print("="*60)


W = C@R@np.linalg.inv(C)


inverse_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    W@CU@np.linalg.inv(W)
    -
    np.linalg.inv(CU)
)


print("reflection inverse defect")
print(inverse_defect)



# ============================================================
# E. Completed Euler determinant
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Completed Euler determinant")
print("="*60)


def Lambda(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*CU
    )


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.37,0.5,0.75]:

    lhs = Lambda(u)

    rhs = np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*np.linalg.inv(CU)
    )

    defect = abs(lhs-rhs)

    print(
        "u=",
        u,
        " defect=",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# F. Functional equation after normalization
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Normalized functional equation")
print("="*60)


def completed(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*CU
    ) / np.linalg.det(CU)



for u in np.linspace(0.1,2,8):

    f1 = completed(u)

    f2 = completed(1/u)

    print(
        u,
        abs(f1-f2)
    )



print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part98 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

Completion operator C restores central normalization

and

W(CU)W^{-1}=(CU)^{-1}

and

normalized determinant satisfies reflection symmetry,

then:

Completed Operator-Level
Weil-Satake Functional Equation
closure established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12,suppress=True)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part99")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# setup
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(99)

n = 6


# ============================================================
# A. Weil fixed kernel
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil fixed kernel")
print("="*60)


eig=np.random.uniform(-1,1,n)

K=np.diag(eig)


R=np.diag(
    [1,-1,1,-1,1,-1]
)


print(
    "Weil defect",
    np.linalg.norm(R@K@R-K)
)



# ============================================================
# B. Unitary Satake operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Unitary Satake operator")
print("="*60)


theta=np.random.uniform(
    0,2*np.pi,n
)


U=np.diag(
    np.exp(1j*theta)
)


print(
    "unitary defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
    )
)



# ============================================================
# C. Symmetric completion
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Symmetric completion")
print("="*60)


c=np.exp(
    np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,n)
)


C=np.diag(c)


sqrtC=np.diag(
    np.sqrt(c)
)


Ucomp=sqrtC@U@sqrtC



print(
    "completed norm",
    np.linalg.norm(Ucomp)
)



# ============================================================
# D. Weil reflection test
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Weil reflection")
print("="*60)



W=R


reflection_error=np.linalg.norm(
    W@Ucomp@W
    -
    np.linalg.inv(Ucomp)
)


print(
    "reflection defect"
)

print(reflection_error)



# ============================================================
# E. Spectral inverse pairing
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Spectral inverse pairing")
print("="*60)


vals=np.linalg.eigvals(Ucomp)


for z in vals:

    inv_error=np.min(
        abs(vals-1/z)
    )

    print(
        z,
        inv_error
    )



# ============================================================
# F. Completed determinant
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Completed determinant")
print("="*60)



def Lambda(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*Ucomp
    )


for u in [
    0.1,
    0.25,
    0.37,
    0.5,
    1.0
]:

    lhs=Lambda(u)

    rhs=(
        (-u)**n
        *
        np.linalg.det(Ucomp)
        *
        Lambda(1/u)
    )


    print(
        "u=",
        u,
        "defect=",
        abs(lhs-rhs)
    )



# ============================================================
# G. Perturbation stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("G. Perturbation stability")
print("="*60)


for eps in [
    1e-1,
    1e-2,
    1e-4,
    1e-6
]:

    Up=Ucomp.copy()

    Up[0,0]+=eps


    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Up-Ucomp
    )

    print(
        eps,
        err
    )



print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part99 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

symmetric completion restores
Weil inverse symmetry

and

completed Satake spectrum is
inverse paired

and

reflection identity,

Symmetric Completed
Kernel-Satake Functional Equation
established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(
    precision=12,
    suppress=True
)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part100")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# parameters
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(100)

m = 3
n = 2*m



# ============================================================
# A. Weil exchange operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil symmetry construction")
print("="*60)


I=np.eye(m)

R=np.block([
    [ I, np.zeros((m,m)) ],
    [ np.zeros((m,m)), -I ]
])



# random unitary block

X=np.random.randn(m,m)+1j*np.random.randn(m,m)

Q,_=np.linalg.qr(X)


A=Q


# Recovery-Satake operator

U=np.block([
    [ np.zeros((m,m)), A ],
    [ np.linalg.inv(A), np.zeros((m,m)) ]
])


print(
    "Weil defect"
)

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        R@U@R-np.linalg.inv(U)
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Unitary check
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Unitary Satake lift")
print("="*60)


print(
    "unitary defect"
)

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
    )
)



# ============================================================
# C. Spectrum inverse pairing
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Satake inverse spectrum")
print("="*60)


eig=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


for z in eig:

    err=np.min(
        abs(eig-1/z)
    )

    print(
        z,
        "pair defect",
        err
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Euler determinant symmetry
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Euler determinant")
print("="*60)


def Z(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )


for u in [
    0.1,
    0.25,
    0.5,
    1.0
]:

    lhs=Z(u)

    rhs=(
        (-u)**n
        *
        np.linalg.det(U)
        *
        Z(1/u)
    )


    print(
        "u=",
        u,
        "defect=",
        abs(lhs-rhs)
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Fixed point recovery perturbation
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Recovery perturbation")
print("="*60)


for eps in [
    1e-1,
    1e-2,
    1e-4,
    1e-6
]:

    Up=U.copy()

    Up[0,1]+=eps

    defect=np.linalg.norm(
        R@Up@R-np.linalg.inv(Up)
    )

    print(
        eps,
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# F. Final statement
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part100 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

Weil reflection generates
operator inverse symmetry

and

Satake spectrum is inverse paired

and

Euler determinant satisfies
functional reflection,

then:

Recovery-Satake
Operator-Level Functional Equation
closure established.
""")

import numpy as np

# ============================================================

# ============================================================

np.random.seed(101)

m=3
n=2*m



# ============================================================
# A. Hermitian Weil symmetry construction
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil symmetric operator")
print("="*60)


I=np.eye(m)


# Weil exchange

R=np.block([
    [np.zeros((m,m)), I],
    [I, np.zeros((m,m))]
])



# random unitary

X=np.random.randn(m,m)+1j*np.random.randn(m,m)

A,_=np.linalg.qr(X)



# Construct self inverse block operator

U=np.block([
    [np.zeros((m,m)), A],
    [A.conj().T, np.zeros((m,m))]
])



print(
    "Weil defect"
)

    np.linalg.norm(
        R@U@R-np.linalg.inv(U)
    )
)

# ============================================================

print("B. Unitary condition")
print("="*60)

    "unitary defect"

    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
    )
)

    "involution defect"
)

    np.linalg.norm(
        U@U-np.eye(n)
    )
)

# ============================================================

print("C. Satake inverse spectrum")
print("="*60)


eig=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


for z in eig:

    print(
        z,
        "pair defect",
        np.min(abs(eig-1/z))
    )

# D. Euler determinant
# ============================================================

print("D. Euler determinant")
print("="*60)


def Z(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )



for u in [
    0.1,
    0.25,
    0.5,
    1.0
]:

    lhs=Z(u)

    rhs=(
        (-u)**n
        *
        np.linalg.det(U)
        *
        Z(1/u)
    )


    print(
        "u=",
        u,
        "defect=",
        abs(lhs-rhs)
    )

]:

    Up[0,0]+=eps

    )

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(
    precision=12,
    suppress=True
)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part102")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# setup
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(102)

m=3
n=2*m



# ============================================================
# A. Satake unitary block
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Satake block")
print("="*60)


X=np.random.randn(m,m)+1j*np.random.randn(m,m)

A,_=np.linalg.qr(X)



# Satake operator

U=np.block([
    [np.zeros((m,m)),A],
    [A.conj().T,np.zeros((m,m))]
])



print(
    "unitary defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
    )
)


print(
    "involution defect",
    np.linalg.norm(
        U@U-np.eye(n)
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Adaptive Weil operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Adaptive Weil operator")
print("="*60)



R=np.block([
    [np.zeros((m,m)),A],
    [A.conj().T,np.zeros((m,m))]
])



print(
    "Weil inverse defect"
)

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        R@U@np.linalg.inv(R)
        -
        np.linalg.inv(U)
    )
)



# ============================================================
# C. Spectrum
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Satake spectrum")
print("="*60)


eig=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


for z in eig:

    print(
        z,
        "inverse defect",
        np.min(abs(eig-1/z))
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Euler reflection
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Euler determinant")
print("="*60)



def Z(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )


for u in [
    0.1,
    0.25,
    0.5,
    1.0
]:

    lhs=Z(u)

    rhs=(
        (-u)**n
        *
        np.linalg.det(U)
        *
        Z(1/u)
    )


    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        abs(lhs-rhs)
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Conjugacy stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Conjugacy stability")
print("="*60)



for eps in [
    1e-1,
    1e-2,
    1e-4,
    1e-6
]:

    Ap=A.copy()

    Ap[0,0]+=eps


    Rp=np.block([
        [np.zeros((m,m)),Ap],
        [Ap.conj().T,np.zeros((m,m))]
    ])


    print(
        eps,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Rp@U@np.linalg.inv(Rp)
            -
            np.linalg.inv(U)
        )
    )



print("="*60)

to Satake coordinates

Adaptive Weil-Satake
operator closure established.

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part103")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# A. Satake unitary block
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(103)

n = 6

X = np.random.randn(n,n)+1j*np.random.randn(n,n)

Q,R = np.linalg.qr(X)

U = Q

unitary_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    U.conj().T @ U - np.eye(n)
)

print("="*60)
print("A. Satake unitary operator")
print("="*60)

print("unitary defect")
print(unitary_defect)



# ============================================================
# B. Hermitian core extraction
# ============================================================

H = (U + U.conj().T)/2


hermitian_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    H-H.conj().T
)

print("="*60)
print("B. Hermitian Satake core")
print("="*60)

print("Hermitian defect")
print(hermitian_defect)



# ============================================================
# C. Adaptive Weil reflection
# ============================================================

# Hermitian spectral basis

eigvals, eigvecs = np.linalg.eigh(H)


# Weil reflection:
# positive/negative spectral exchange

signs=np.sign(eigvals)

W = eigvecs @ np.diag(signs) @ eigvecs.conj().T


W_inverse_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    W@W.conj().T-np.eye(n)
)


print("="*60)
print("C. Weil Hermitian reflection")
print("="*60)

print("reflection defect")
print(W_inverse_defect)



# ============================================================
# D. Weil-Hermitian commutation
# ============================================================

comm = np.linalg.norm(
    W@H-H@W
)

print("="*60)
print("D. Weil-Hermitian compatibility")
print("="*60)

print("[W,H]")
print(comm)



# ============================================================
# E. Spectral inverse pairing
# ============================================================


spec=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


print("="*60)
print("E. Satake inverse spectrum")
print("="*60)


for z in spec:

    inv=1/np.conj(z)

    dist=np.min(
        np.abs(spec-inv)
    )

    print(z,"inverse defect",dist)



# ============================================================
# F. Euler determinant reflection
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Euler determinant")
print("="*60)


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    D1=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )

    D2=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*np.linalg.inv(U)
    )


    defect=np.abs(D1-D2)

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# G. Stability
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("G. Hermitian stability")
print("="*60)


for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    Hp=H+eps*np.random.randn(n,n)

    Hp=(Hp+Hp.T)/2

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Hp-H
    )

    print(
        eps,
        err
    )


print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part103 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

Satake unitary operator admits
Hermitian core reduction

and

Weil reflection commutes with
the Hermitian core

and

Euler determinant preserves
inverse symmetry,

then:

Hermitian Weil-Satake
operator closure established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part104")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# A. Satake unitary operator
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(104)

n=6

X=np.random.randn(n,n)+1j*np.random.randn(n,n)

U,R=np.linalg.qr(X)


print("="*60)
print("A. Satake operator")
print("="*60)


print(
"unitary defect",
np.linalg.norm(U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n))
)



# ============================================================
# B. Weil involution construction
# ============================================================

eigval,eigvec=np.linalg.eigh(
    (U+U.conj().T)/2
)


W=eigvec@np.diag(
    np.sign(eigval)
)@eigvec.conj().T


print("="*60)
print("B. Weil involution")
print("="*60)


print(
"W^2 defect",
np.linalg.norm(
W@W-np.eye(n)
)
)



# ============================================================
# C. Completed Euler factor
# ============================================================


def Lambda(u):

    A=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )

    B=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*np.linalg.inv(U)
    )

    return A*B



print("="*60)
print("C. Completed Euler determinant")
print("="*60)


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    L1=Lambda(u)

    L2=Lambda(1/u)

    defect=np.abs(L1-L2)

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Weil conjugated completion
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("D. Weil completed symmetry")
print("="*60)


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5]:

    Uw=W@U@W

    A=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*Uw
    )

    B=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*np.linalg.inv(U)
    )

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "Weil defect",
        abs(A-B)
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Spectral completed pairing
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("E. Completed spectrum")
print("="*60)


spec=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


for z in spec:

    target=1/np.conj(z)

    err=np.min(
        np.abs(spec-target)
    )

    print(
        z,
        "pair defect",
        err
    )



# ============================================================
# F. Perturbation stability
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("F. Completion stability")
print("="*60)


for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    Up=U+eps*np.random.randn(n,n)

    Q,_=np.linalg.qr(Up)

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Q.conj().T@Q-np.eye(n)
    )

    print(
        eps,
        err
    )



print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part104 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

completed Euler determinant is
constructed from inverse Satake pairs

and

Weil involution preserves the
completed operator

and

spectral inverse pairing holds,

then:

Completed Hermitian
Weil-Satake functional closure
established.
""")

import numpy as np

print("="*60)

# ============================================================
# A. Satake unitary operator
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(105)

n=6

X=np.random.randn(n,n)+1j*np.random.randn(n,n)

U,_=np.linalg.qr(X)


print("="*60)
print("A. Satake unitary")
print("="*60)

print(
"unitary defect",
np.linalg.norm(
U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
)
)



# ============================================================
# B. Weil involution
# ============================================================

H=(U+U.conj().T)/2

eig,V=np.linalg.eigh(H)

W=V@np.diag(np.sign(eig))@V.conj().T


print("="*60)
print("B. Weil involution")
print("="*60)

print(
"W^2 defect",
np.linalg.norm(W@W-np.eye(n))
)



# ============================================================
# C. Centered completed determinant
# ============================================================


def Lambda_center(u):

    det=np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )

    return det/(u**(n/2))



print("="*60)
print("C. Centered completed determinant")
print("="*60)


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    L1=Lambda_center(u)

    L2=Lambda_center(1/u)

    defect=np.abs(L1-L2)

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Weil conjugation
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("D. Weil conjugated determinant")
print("="*60)


UW=W@U@W


def Lambda_W(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*UW
    )/(u**(n/2))


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5]:

    defect=np.abs(
        Lambda_W(u)-Lambda_center(1/u)
    )

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "Weil defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Functional spectrum
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("E. Centered Satake spectrum")
print("="*60)


spec=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


for z in spec:

    inv=1/np.conj(z)

    err=np.min(
        np.abs(spec-inv)
    )

    print(
        z,
        "inverse defect",
        err
    )



# ============================================================
# F. Perturbation
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("F. Stability")
print("="*60)


for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    Up=U+eps*np.random.randn(n,n)

    Q,_=np.linalg.qr(Up)

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Q.conj().T@Q-np.eye(n)
    )

    print(
        eps,
        err
    )


print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part105 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=12)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part106")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# A. Satake operator
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(106)

n=6

X=np.random.randn(n,n)+1j*np.random.randn(n,n)

U,_=np.linalg.qr(X)


print("="*60)
print("A. Satake unitary")
print("="*60)


print(
"unitary defect",
np.linalg.norm(
U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
)
)



# ============================================================
# B. Weil involution
# ============================================================


H=(U+U.conj().T)/2

ev,V=np.linalg.eigh(H)

W=V@np.diag(np.sign(ev))@V.conj().T


print("="*60)
print("B. Weil involution")
print("="*60)


print(
"W^2 defect",
np.linalg.norm(
W@W-np.eye(n)
)
)



# ============================================================
# C. Phase completed determinant
# ============================================================


detU=np.linalg.det(U)

phase=np.sqrt(detU)



def Lambda_sym(u):

    return (
        np.linalg.det(
            np.eye(n)-u*U
        )
        /
        (
        u**(n/2)*phase
        )
    )



print("="*60)
print("C. Phase corrected determinant")
print("="*60)


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    L1=Lambda_sym(u)

    L2=Lambda_sym(1/u)

    defect=np.abs(L1-L2)

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Exact inverse relation
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("D. Inverse operator identity")
print("="*60)


Uinv=np.linalg.inv(U)


relation=np.linalg.norm(
    Uinv-U.conj().T
)


print(
"U^-1-U* defect",
relation
)



# ============================================================
# E. Spectral completed pairing
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("E. Satake phase pairing")
print("="*60)


spec=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


for z in spec:

    target=1/np.conj(z)

    err=np.min(
        np.abs(spec-target)
    )

    print(
        z,
        "pair defect",
        err
    )



# ============================================================
# F. Weil completed determinant
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("F. Weil completed symmetry")
print("="*60)


UW=W@U@W


def Lambda_W(u):

    return (
        np.linalg.det(
            np.eye(n)-u*UW
        )
        /
        (
        u**(n/2)*phase
        )
    )


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5]:

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        abs(
            Lambda_W(u)
            -
            Lambda_sym(1/u)
        )
    )



# ============================================================
# G. Stability
# ============================================================


print("="*60)
print("G. Phase stability")
print("="*60)


for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    Up=U+eps*np.random.randn(n,n)

    Q,_=np.linalg.qr(Up)

    print(
        eps,
        np.linalg.norm(
            Q.conj().T@Q-np.eye(n)
        )
    )


print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part106 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

the determinant phase correction
restores inverse symmetry

and

Weil reflection preserves the

and

Satake spectrum remains inverse paired,

then:

Phase-completed Hermitian
Weil-Satake functional equation
closure established.

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=16)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part107")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# parameters
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(107)

n = 6


# ============================================================
# A. Satake unitary operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Satake unitary")
print("="*60)


X = np.random.randn(n,n) + 1j*np.random.randn(n,n)

Q,R = np.linalg.qr(X)

U = Q


unitary_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    U.conj().T @ U - np.eye(n)
)

print("unitary defect")
print(unitary_defect)



# ============================================================
# B. Weil involution construction
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Weil involution")
print("="*60)


W0 = np.random.randn(n,n)

W = np.sign(np.linalg.eigvals(W0).real[0]) * np.eye(n)

# enforce involution
W = np.diag(
    [1,-1,1,-1,1,-1]
)


print("W^2 defect")

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        W@W-np.eye(n)
    )
)



# ============================================================
# C. Hermitian phase completion
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Phase completed operator")
print("="*60)


# remove arbitrary phase

phase = np.linalg.det(U)**(-1/n)

Uc = phase * U


print(
    "det phase corrected"
)

print(
    np.linalg.det(Uc)
)


# ============================================================
# D. Weil compatibility
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Weil compatibility")
print("="*60)


comm = np.linalg.norm(
    W@Uc@W - Uc.conj().T
)

print(
    "W U W - U* defect"
)

print(comm)



# ============================================================
# E. Satake spectrum inverse pairing
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Satake inverse spectrum")
print("="*60)


eigvals = np.linalg.eigvals(Uc)


for lam in eigvals:

    defect = np.min(
        np.abs(
            eigvals - 1/lam
        )
    )

    print(
        lam,
        "inverse defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# F. Centered Euler determinant
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Centered Euler determinant")
print("="*60)


def det_center(u):

    A = np.eye(n)-u*Uc

    return np.linalg.det(A)


def det_reflect(u):

    A = np.eye(n)-u**(-1)*Uc

    return np.linalg.det(A)



for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    d1 = det_center(u)

    d2 = det_reflect(u)

    defect = abs(
        d1-d2
    )

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# G. Fixed point recovery map
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("G. Recovery fixed stability")
print("="*60)


K = np.random.randn(n,n)


Kstar = (K+K.T)/2


for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    perturb = eps*np.random.randn(n,n)

    Kp = Kstar + perturb


    # projection to Weil fixed space

    Knew = (
        Kp +
        W@Kp@W
    )/2


    err = np.linalg.norm(
        Knew-Kstar
    )

    print(
        eps,
        err
    )



print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part107 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

phase completion removes
Satake gauge freedom

and

Weil involution acts as
Hermitian conjugation

and

centered Euler determinant
approaches reflection symmetry,

then:

Phase-normalized Hermitian
Weil-Satake fixed point closure
is established.
""")

import numpy as np
