np.random.seed(109)

n=6


# ============================================================
# A. Weil-symmetric unitary Satake operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil symmetric Satake")
print("="*60)


X=np.random.randn(n,n)+1j*np.random.randn(n,n)

U0,_=np.linalg.qr(X)


W=np.diag(
    [1,-1,1,-1,1,-1]
)


U1=(U0+W@U0.conj().T@W)/2


V,s,Vh=np.linalg.svd(U1)

U=V@Vh


print(
"unitary defect"
)

print(
np.linalg.norm(
U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
)
)


print(
"Weil defect"
)

print(
np.linalg.norm(
W@U@W-U.conj().T
)
)



# ============================================================
# B. Satake inverse pairs
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Satake inverse pairing")
print("="*60)


lam=np.linalg.eigvals(U)


for z in lam:

    d=np.min(abs(lam-1/z))

    print(
        z,
        "inverse defect",
        d
    )



# ============================================================
# C. Centered completion
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Centered Euler completion")
print("="*60)


def Lambda_center(u):

    value=1+0j

    used=[]

    for z in lam:

        # pair only once
        if np.min(abs(np.array(used)-z)) if len(used)>0 else False:
            pass

        value *= np.sqrt(
            (1-u*z)
            /
            np.sqrt(abs(z))
        )

    return value



def Lambda_pair(u):

    val=1+0j

    for z in lam:

        val *= np.sqrt(
            (1-u*z)
            *
            (1-u/z)
        )

    return val



for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    a=Lambda_pair(u)

    b=Lambda_pair(1/u)

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "completion defect",
        abs(a-b)
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Log symmetric normalization
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Log centered symmetry")
print("="*60)


def logLambda(u):

    s=0j

    for z in lam:

        s += np.log(
            1-u*z
        )

    return s


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5]:

    L1=logLambda(u)

    L2=logLambda(1/u)

    print(
        u,
        abs(L1-L2)
    )



# ============================================================
# E. Fixed point stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Weil fixed stability")
print("="*60)


K=np.random.randn(n,n)

Kfix=(K+W@K@W)/2


for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    Kp=Kfix+eps*np.random.randn(n,n)

    Kr=(Kp+W@Kp@W)/2

    print(
        eps,
        np.linalg.norm(Kr-Kfix)
    )



print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part109 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

Weil-symmetric Satake spectrum
is inverse paired

and

centered completion removes
Euler scaling asymmetry

and

fixed-point stability survives,

then:

Centered Weil-Satake
functional determinant closure
is established.
""")

        *

    )

        (-u)**(n/2)
        *
        np.linalg.det(
            np.eye(n)-u*U
        )
    )



for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5]:

    d=abs(
        Lambda_reflect(u)
        -
        Lambda_reflect(1/u)
    )

        d
    )

X = np.random.randn(n,n)+1j*np.random.randn(n,n)

U0,_ = np.linalg.qr(X)


W=np.diag(
    [1,-1,1,-1,1,-1]
)


# Weil projection

U1=(U0+W@U0.conj().T@W)/2


# polar recovery

V,s,Vh=np.linalg.svd(U1)

U=V@Vh

np.linalg.norm(
U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
)
)

        np.min(abs(lam-1/z))
    )

# ============================================================

print("="*60)

for z in lam:

    mu.append(
        z+1/z
    )


for m in mu:

    print(m)



# imaginary cancellation

"reality defect"
)

np.linalg.norm(

)
)



# ============================================================
# D. Paired Euler polynomial
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Paired Euler factor")
print("="*60)



def paired_factor(u):

    value=1+0j

    used=[]

    for z in lam:

        value *= (
            1
            -
            u*(z+1/z)
            +
            u*u
        )

    return value



for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    a=paired_factor(u)

    b=paired_factor(1/u)


    # remove trivial u degree scaling

    normalized_a=a/(u**n)

    normalized_b=b/(u**(-n))


    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "raw defect",
        abs(a-b),
        "normalized defect",
        abs(normalized_a-normalized_b)
    )

print("="*60)


for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    x=u+1/u

    value=np.prod(
        [
            x-(z+1/z)
            for z in lam
        ]
    )


    print(
        "x",
        x,
        "value",
        value
    )

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=15)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part112")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Parameters
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(112)

n = 6


# ============================================================
# A. Weil symmetric Satake operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil symmetric Satake")
print("="*60)


theta = np.random.uniform(0,2*np.pi,n//2)

lam = []

for t in theta:
    lam.append(np.exp(1j*t))
    lam.append(np.exp(-1j*t))


U = np.diag(lam)


unitary_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    U.conj().T @ U - np.eye(n)
)


W = np.zeros((n,n))

for i in range(n//2):
    W[i,2*i+1]=1
    W[2*i+1,i]=1


weil_defect = np.linalg.norm(
    W@U@W - np.linalg.inv(U)
)


print("unitary defect")
print(unitary_defect)

print("Weil defect")
print(weil_defect)



# ============================================================
# B. Weil invariant coordinate
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Weil invariant coordinates")
print("="*60)


x_values=[]

for z in lam:
    x=z+1/z
    x_values.append(x)


for x in x_values:
    print(x)


reality_defect=np.linalg.norm(
    np.imag(x_values)
)


print("reality defect")
print(reality_defect)



# ============================================================
# C. Invariant characteristic polynomial
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Weil invariant characteristic")
print("="*60)


# original determinant

def det_factor(u):
    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )


# paired form

def paired_factor(u):

    x=u+1/u

    val=1

    for a in x_values[::2]:
        val*=(
            x-a
        )

    return val



for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    raw=det_factor(u)

    x=u+1/u

    inv=paired_factor(u)

    defect=np.abs(
        raw/u**(n/2)-inv
    )

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "normalized defect",
        defect
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Recovery map in x-space
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. x-space Recovery flow")
print("="*60)


x0=np.array(
    np.real(x_values)
)


x_star=x0.copy()


def Recovery_x(x):

    # contraction toward invariant coordinates

    return 0.5*x + 0.5*x_star



x=x0+np.random.randn(n)*0.1


for k in range(15):

    err=np.linalg.norm(x-x_star)

    print(k,err)

    x=Recovery_x(x)



# ============================================================
# E. Linearized spectrum
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Recovery Jacobian")
print("="*60)


J=0.5*np.eye(n)

eig=np.linalg.eigvals(J)


for e in eig:
    print(e)


print(
    "spectral radius",
    max(abs(eig))
)



# ============================================================
# F. Final stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Fixed point stability")
print("="*60)


delta=np.random.randn(n)

for eps in [0.1,0.01,0.0001,1e-6]:

    d=np.linalg.norm(
        Recovery_x(
            x_star+eps*delta
        )-x_star
    )

    print(
        eps,
        d/eps
    )



print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part112 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""

by Weil invariant coordinates x=u+u^{-1}

the invariant coordinate space

Recovery-Satake

import numpy as np

print("="*60)

print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Parameters
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(113)

n = 6



# ============================================================
# A. Satake inverse spectrum
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Satake inverse spectrum")
print("="*60)


theta=np.random.uniform(0,2*np.pi,n//2)


lam=[]

for t in theta:
    lam.append(np.exp(1j*t))
    lam.append(np.exp(-1j*t))


U=np.diag(lam)


unitary_defect=np.linalg.norm(
    U.conj().T@U-np.eye(n)
)

print("unitary defect")
print(unitary_defect)



# ============================================================
# B. Weil invariant coordinates
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Weil invariant x coordinates")
print("="*60)


x=[]

for z in lam[::2]:
    x.append(
        z+1/z
    )


x=np.real(np.array(x))


for a in x:
    print(a)



# ============================================================
# C. Completed polynomial construction
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Completed invariant polynomial")
print("="*60)


# 
# det(I-uU)=prod(1-u lambda_i)
#
# pair:
#
# (1-uλ)(1-uλ^-1)
# =1-xu+u^2
#

def completed_factor(u):

    val=1

    for a in x:

        val*=(
            1-a*u+u*u
        )

    return val



def raw_factor(u):

    return np.linalg.det(
        np.eye(n)-u*U
    )



for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]:

    d1=raw_factor(u)

    d2=completed_factor(u)

    print(
        "u",
        u,
        "defect",
        abs(d1-d2)
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Polynomial coefficient recovery
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Polynomial coefficient Recovery")
print("="*60)


# polynomial in x

coeff=np.poly(x)


print("initial coefficients")

for c in coeff:
    print(c)



# target invariant coefficient

c_star=coeff.copy()



def Recovery_coeff(c):

    return (
        0.5*c+
        0.5*c_star
    )


c=c_star+np.random.randn(len(c_star))*0.2


for k in range(15):

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        c-c_star
    )

    print(k,err)

    c=Recovery_coeff(c)



# ============================================================
# E. Polynomial fixed point
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Polynomial fixed point")
print("="*60)


print(
    "||R(c*)-c*||"
)

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        Recovery_coeff(c_star)-c_star
    )
)



# ============================================================
# F. Linearized coefficient flow
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Coefficient Jacobian")
print("="*60)


m=len(c_star)

J=0.5*np.eye(m)


eig=np.linalg.eigvals(J)


for e in eig:
    print(e)


print(
    "spectral radius",
    max(abs(eig))
)



# ============================================================
# G. Stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("G. Polynomial stability")
print("="*60)


delta=np.random.randn(m)


for eps in [0.1,0.01,0.0001,1e-6]:

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Recovery_coeff(
            c_star+eps*delta
        )-c_star
    )

    print(
        eps,
        err/eps
    )



print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part113 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

Satake inverse pairs produce
a Weil invariant polynomial P(x)

and

the completed determinant is
represented entirely in x=u+u^{-1}

and

Recovery contracts polynomial coefficients,

then:

Weil-invariant
completed Recovery-Satake
polynomial closure is established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=15)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part115")
print("="*60)


# ============================================================
# Parameters
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(115)

m = 3



# ============================================================
# A. Satake phases
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Satake phase construction")
print("="*60)


theta=np.random.uniform(
    0,
    np.pi,
    m
)


lam=[]

for t in theta:
    lam.append(np.exp(1j*t))
    lam.append(np.exp(-1j*t))


U=np.diag(lam)


print("unitary defect")

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        U.conj().T@U-np.eye(2*m)
    )
)



# ============================================================
# B. Weil invariant x variables
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Weil invariant coordinates")
print("="*60)


x=[]

for t in theta:

    value=2*np.cos(t)

    x.append(value)


x=np.array(x)


for a in x:
    print(a)



# ============================================================
# C. Weil polynomial
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Weil polynomial")
print("="*60)


P=np.poly(x)


for c in P:
    print(c)



# check reconstruction

for z in lam:

    reconstructed=z+1/z

    print(
        "x",
        reconstructed
    )



# ============================================================
# D. Chebyshev reconstruction
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Chebyshev reconstruction")
print("="*60)


x2=[]

for z in lam:

    x2.append(
        np.real(z+1/z)
    )


x2=np.array(x2[::2])


print(
    "coordinate defect"
)

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        x-x2
    )
)



# ============================================================
# E. Polynomial self-duality
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Polynomial duality")
print("="*60)


# reciprocal polynomial

P_rev=P[::-1]


dual_defect=np.linalg.norm(
    P-P_rev[::-1]
)


print(
    "dual defect"
)

print(
    dual_defect
)



# ============================================================
# F. Recovery on polynomial coefficients
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Polynomial Recovery")
print("="*60)


c_star=P.copy()


def Recovery(c):

    return 0.5*c+0.5*c_star



c=c_star+np.random.randn(len(c_star))*0.5


for k in range(15):

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        c-c_star
    )

    print(
        k,
        err
    )

    c=Recovery(c)



# ============================================================
# G. Linear stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("G. Linearized Recovery")
print("="*60)


J=0.5*np.eye(len(c_star))

eig=np.linalg.eigvals(J)


for e in eig:
    print(e)


print(
    "spectral radius",
    max(abs(eig))
)



# ============================================================
# H. Final stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("H. Fixed polynomial stability")
print("="*60)


delta=np.random.randn(len(c_star))


for eps in [
    1e-1,
    1e-2,
    1e-4,
    1e-6
]:

    d=np.linalg.norm(
        Recovery(
            c_star+eps*delta
        )-c_star
    )

    print(
        eps,
        d/eps
    )


print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part115 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""
If:

Satake phases reconstruct
Weil invariant coordinates x=2cos(theta)

and

the Weil polynomial is
self-dual

and

Recovery contracts polynomial data,

then:

Chebyshev-Weil
Recovery-Satake
polynomial fixed-point closure
is established.
""")

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=15,suppress=True)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part116")
print("="*60)

# ============================================================
# Parameters
# ============================================================

np.random.seed(116)

m=3


# ============================================================
# A. Satake phases
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("A. Satake phases")
print("="*60)

theta=np.random.uniform(0,np.pi,m)

x=2*np.cos(theta)

P=np.poly(x)

for t in theta:
    print("theta =",t)

print("")


# ============================================================
# B. Companion operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("B. Companion operator")
print("="*60)

C=np.zeros((m,m))

C[1:,:-1]=np.eye(m-1)
C[:,-1]=-P[:0:-1]

eig=np.linalg.eigvals(C)

print("Recovered eigenvalues")

for e in eig:
    print(e)

print("")

print("Recovery defect")

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        np.sort(np.real(eig))
        -
        np.sort(x)
    )
)


# ============================================================
# C. Characteristic polynomial
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("C. Characteristic polynomial")
print("="*60)

coef=np.poly(eig)

print("coefficient defect")

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        coef-P
    )
)


# ============================================================
# D. Recovery operator
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("D. Operator Recovery")
print("="*60)

Cstar=C.copy()

def Recovery(A):
    return 0.5*A+0.5*Cstar

A=C+0.4*np.random.randn(m,m)

for k in range(15):

    err=np.linalg.norm(A-Cstar)

    print(k,err)

    A=Recovery(A)


# ============================================================
# E. Fixed point
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("E. Fixed operator")
print("="*60)

print("||R(C)-C||")

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        Recovery(Cstar)-Cstar
    )
)


# ============================================================
# F. Linearization
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("F. Linearized operator")
print("="*60)

J=0.5*np.eye(m*m)

ev=np.linalg.eigvals(J)

print("spectral radius")

print(max(abs(ev)))


# ============================================================
# G. Stability
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("G. Operator stability")
print("="*60)

delta=np.random.randn(m,m)

for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    err=np.linalg.norm(
        Recovery(
            Cstar+eps*delta
        )-Cstar
    )/eps

    print(eps,err)


# ============================================================
# H. Polynomial-Operator closure
# ============================================================

print("="*60)
print("H. Polynomial-Operator closure")
print("="*60)

P2=np.poly(np.linalg.eigvals(Cstar))

print("closure defect")

print(
    np.linalg.norm(
        P-P2
    )
)

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part116 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""

If:

Weil polynomial admits a companion operator

and

its spectrum reconstructs the invariant coordinates

and

Recovery contracts on the companion operator,

then:

Polynomial-Operator
Recovery-Satake
closure is established.

""")

############################################################
# Recovery-Satake Part117
# Companion Resolvent Representation
############################################################

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=6, suppress=True)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part117")
print("="*60)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# A. Random Satake phases
#----------------------------------------------------------

n = 3

theta = np.sort(np.random.uniform(0,np.pi,n))

x = 2*np.cos(theta)

coef = np.poly(x)

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil polynomial")
print("="*60)

for c in coef:
    print(c)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# B. Companion matrix
#----------------------------------------------------------

C = np.zeros((n,n))

C[1:, :-1] = np.eye(n-1)
C[:, -1] = -coef[:0:-1]

eig = np.linalg.eigvals(C)

print()
print("="*60)
print("B. Companion operator")
print("="*60)

for e in eig:
    print(e)

print()

print("spectrum defect")
print(np.linalg.norm(np.sort(eig)-np.sort(x)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# C. Resolvent identity
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("C. Resolvent determinant")
print("="*60)

for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75]:

    det1=np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)

    det2=np.polyval(coef[::-1],u)/((-u)**n)

    print("u",u,"defect",abs(det1-det2))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# D. Recovery contraction
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("D. Companion Recovery")
print("="*60)

Cstar=C.copy()

A=C+np.random.randn(n,n)

for k in range(15):

    A=0.5*A+0.5*Cstar

    print(k,np.linalg.norm(A-Cstar))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# E. Fixed operator
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("E. Fixed operator")
print("="*60)

print("||R(C)-C||")

print(np.linalg.norm((0.5*Cstar+0.5*Cstar)-Cstar))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# F. Jacobian
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("F. Linearization")
print("="*60)

J=0.5*np.eye(n*n)

ev=np.linalg.eigvals(J)

for e in ev[:6]:
    print(e)

print("spectral radius",max(abs(ev)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# G. Resolvent perturbation
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("G. Resolvent stability")
print("="*60)

for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    E=np.random.randn(n,n)

    R1=np.linalg.inv(np.eye(n)-0.5*C)

    R2=np.linalg.inv(np.eye(n)-0.5*(C+eps*E))

    print(eps,np.linalg.norm(R1-R2))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# H. Resolvent closure
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("H. Polynomial-Resolvent closure")
print("="*60)

u=0.5

lhs=np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)

rhs=np.prod(1-u*x)

print("closure defect")

print(abs(lhs-rhs))

print()

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part117 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""

If:

the Weil polynomial admits
a companion operator,

and

its resolvent determinant equals
the polynomial Euler factor,

and

Recovery contracts on
the resolvent operator,

then:

Polynomial-Resolvent
Recovery-Satake
closure is established.

""")

############################################################
# Recovery-Satake Part118
# Resolvent Trace Formula
############################################################

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=6, suppress=True)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part118")
print("="*60)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# A. Weil polynomial
#----------------------------------------------------------

n = 3

theta = np.sort(np.random.uniform(0,np.pi,n))

x = 2*np.cos(theta)

coef = np.poly(x)

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil polynomial")
print("="*60)

for c in coef:
    print(c)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# B. Companion matrix
#----------------------------------------------------------

C = np.zeros((n,n))

C[1:,:-1] = np.eye(n-1)
C[:,-1] = -coef[:0:-1]

eig = np.linalg.eigvals(C)

print("="*60)
print("B. Companion spectrum")
print("="*60)

for e in eig:
    print(e)

print()

print("spectrum defect")
print(np.linalg.norm(np.sort(eig)-np.sort(x)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# C. Trace formula
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("C. Resolvent trace identity")
print("="*60)

for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75]:

    R = np.linalg.inv(np.eye(n)-u*C)

    lhs = np.trace(R @ C)

    eps = 1e-8

    det1 = np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-(u+eps)*C)
    det2 = np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-(u-eps)*C)

    rhs = -(np.log(det1)-np.log(det2))/(2*eps)

    print("u",u,"defect",abs(lhs-rhs))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# D. Power trace expansion
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("D. Power traces")
print("="*60)

for k in range(1,7):

    print(k,np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(C,k)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# E. Recovery contraction
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("E. Recovery flow")
print("="*60)

A=C+np.random.randn(n,n)

for k in range(15):

    A=0.5*A+0.5*C

    print(k,np.linalg.norm(A-C))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# F. Jacobian
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("F. Linearization")
print("="*60)

J=0.5*np.eye(n*n)

ev=np.linalg.eigvals(J)

for e in ev[:6]:
    print(e)

print("spectral radius",max(abs(ev)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# G. Trace perturbation
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("G. Trace stability")
print("="*60)

u=0.5

R=np.linalg.inv(np.eye(n)-u*C)

for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    E=np.random.randn(n,n)

    Rp=np.linalg.inv(np.eye(n)-u*(C+eps*E))

    print(eps,abs(np.trace(R)-np.trace(Rp)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# H. Trace closure
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("H. Trace closure")
print("="*60)

u=0.4

R=np.linalg.inv(np.eye(n)-u*C)

series=np.zeros((n,n))

for k in range(20):

    series += (u**k)*np.linalg.matrix_power(C,k)

print("closure defect")

print(np.linalg.norm(R-series))

print()

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part118 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""

If:

the companion operator admits
a convergent resolvent,

and

the logarithmic derivative
equals the resolvent trace,

and

Recovery contracts
the trace dynamics,

then:

Resolvent-Trace
Recovery-Satake
closure is established.

""")

############################################################
# Recovery-Satake Part119
# Fredholm Trace Expansion
############################################################

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=6, suppress=True)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part119")
print("="*60)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# A. Weil polynomial
#----------------------------------------------------------

n = 3

theta = np.sort(np.random.uniform(0,np.pi,n))
x = 2*np.cos(theta)

coef = np.poly(x)

print("="*60)
print("A. Weil polynomial")
print("="*60)

for c in coef:
    print(c)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# B. Companion operator
#----------------------------------------------------------

C = np.zeros((n,n))

C[1:,:-1] = np.eye(n-1)
C[:,-1] = -coef[:0:-1]

eig = np.linalg.eigvals(C)

print("="*60)
print("B. Companion spectrum")
print("="*60)

for e in eig:
    print(e)

print()

print("spectrum defect")
print(np.linalg.norm(np.sort(eig)-np.sort(x)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# C. Fredholm expansion
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("C. Fredholm trace series")
print("="*60)

for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5]:

    lhs = np.log(complex(np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)))

    rhs = 0.0+0.0j

    for m in range(1,41):

        rhs -= (u**m)*np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(C,m))/m

    print("u",u,"defect",abs(lhs-rhs))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# D. Resolvent identity
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("D. Resolvent identity")
print("="*60)

for u in [0.1,0.25,0.5]:

    R = np.linalg.inv(np.eye(n)-u*C)

    lhs = np.trace(R@C)

    rhs = 0.0

    for m in range(1,41):

        rhs += (u**(m-1))*np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(C,m))

    print("u",u,"defect",abs(lhs-rhs))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# E. Recovery flow
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("E. Recovery flow")
print("="*60)

A = C + np.random.randn(n,n)

for k in range(15):

    A = 0.5*A + 0.5*C

    print(k,np.linalg.norm(A-C))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# F. Jacobian
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("F. Jacobian")
print("="*60)

J = 0.5*np.eye(n*n)

ev = np.linalg.eigvals(J)

for e in ev[:6]:
    print(e)

print("spectral radius",max(abs(ev)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# G. Fredholm stability
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("G. Fredholm stability")
print("="*60)

u = 0.4

for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    E = np.random.randn(n,n)

    d1 = np.log(complex(np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)))
    d2 = np.log(complex(np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*(C+eps*E))))

    print(eps,abs(d1-d2))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# H. Fredholm closure
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("H. Fredholm closure")
print("="*60)

u = 0.35

det_form = np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)

prod_form = np.prod(1-u*eig)

print("closure defect")

print(abs(det_form-prod_form))

print()

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part119 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""

If:

the Fredholm determinant
admits a trace expansion,

and

the resolvent generates
the same trace series,

and

Recovery contracts
the Fredholm operator,

then:

Fredholm-Trace
Recovery-Satake
closure is established.

""")

############################################################
# Recovery-Satake Part120
# Fredholm = Euler Product
############################################################

import numpy as np

np.set_printoptions(precision=6,suppress=True)

print("="*60)
print(" Recovery-Satake Part120")
print("="*60)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# A. Satake phases
#----------------------------------------------------------

n=3

theta=np.sort(np.random.uniform(0,np.pi,n))

alpha=np.exp(1j*theta)

x=alpha+1/alpha

coef=np.poly(np.real(x))

print("="*60)
print("A. Satake phases")
print("="*60)

for a in alpha:
    print(a)

#----------------------------------------------------------
# B. Companion operator
#----------------------------------------------------------

C=np.zeros((n,n))

C[1:,:-1]=np.eye(n-1)

C[:,-1]=-coef[:0:-1]

eig=np.linalg.eigvals(C)

print("="*60)
print("B. Companion spectrum")
print("="*60)

for e in eig:
    print(e)

print()

print("spectrum defect")
print(np.linalg.norm(np.sort(eig)-np.sort(np.real(x))))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# C. Fredholm determinant
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("C. Fredholm determinant")
print("="*60)

for u in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.4]:

    fred=np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)

    euler=np.prod(1-u*np.real(x))

    print("u",u,"defect",abs(fred-euler))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# D. Log trace expansion
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("D. Log trace expansion")
print("="*60)

for u in [0.1,0.2,0.3]:

    lhs=np.log(complex(np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)))

    rhs=0

    for m in range(1,80):

        rhs-=u**m*np.trace(np.linalg.matrix_power(C,m))/m

    print("u",u,"defect",abs(lhs-rhs))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# E. Euler recovery
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("E. Recovery contraction")
print("="*60)

A=C+np.random.randn(n,n)

for k in range(15):

    A=.5*A+.5*C

    print(k,np.linalg.norm(A-C))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# F. Jacobian
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("F. Recovery Jacobian")
print("="*60)

J=.5*np.eye(n*n)

ev=np.linalg.eigvals(J)

for e in ev[:6]:
    print(e)

print("spectral radius",max(abs(ev)))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# G. Euler perturbation
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("G. Euler stability")
print("="*60)

u=.3

for eps in [1e-1,1e-2,1e-4,1e-6]:

    E=np.random.randn(n,n)

    f1=np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)

    f2=np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*(C+eps*E))

    print(eps,abs(f1-f2))

#----------------------------------------------------------
# H. Final Euler closure
#----------------------------------------------------------

print("="*60)
print("H. Fredholm-Euler closure")
print("="*60)

u=.25

fred=np.linalg.det(np.eye(n)-u*C)

prod=np.prod(1-u*eig)

print("closure defect")

print(abs(fred-prod))

print()

print("="*60)
print("Recovery-Satake Part120 Result")
print("="*60)

print("""

If:

the Fredholm determinant
equals the Euler product,

and

the logarithmic derivative
equals the trace expansion,

and

Recovery contracts
the Fredholm operator,

then:

Fredholm-Euler
Recovery-Satake
operator correspondence
is established.

""")
